piṟavā yākkip periyōṉākiya civaperumāṉukkum, aṟumukac cevvēḷākiya murukaṉukkum, nīlamēṉi neṭiyō ṉākiya tirumālukkum, veḷḷiya caṅkiṉ niṟattiyuṭiya vāliyōṉākiya palatēvaṉukkum uriya tirukkōyilkaḷ innakaraṅkaḷil amintiruntaṉa. iviyaṉṟik koṟṟavi kōyilum, kāmavēḷ kōṭṭamum, cāttaṉ kōyilum, camaṇappaḷḷiyum, pōti aṟavōṉi vaḻipaṭutaṟkuriya putta vikāramum, intiraṉ kōyilum, kāvaṟpūtaṅkaḷiṉ kōyilkaḷum, ittali nakaraṅkaḷil amikkappaṭṭaṉa. innakarattil nikaḻum tiruviḻākkaḷil pala camaya aṟiñarkaḷum kalantukoṇṭu, tam camaya nuṇporuḷkaḷi ikaliṉṟi viritturittārkaḷ. nakaramakkaḷ taṅkaḷiṭiyē camayap piṇakkut tōṉṟātapaṭi ellāriṭattum karuttu vēṟṟumiyiṉṟi aṉpiṉāṟ paḻakiṉārkaḷ.
peruñcelvarkaḷ eḻu nili māṭaṅkaḷil iṉitu vāḻntu vantārkaḷ; cavikkiṉitākiya aṭicili iravalark kaḷittut tāmum uṇṭu makiḻntaṉar; nilāp payaṉ nukarum neṭunili muṟṟamum, teṉṟal vīcum vēṉiṟpaḷḷiyum, kūtirk kālattu vāṭi pukāta kūtirppaḷḷiyum ākiya vāḻkkic cūḻalkaḷit tam maṉiyiṉkaṇṇē naṉku amittup paruva nilikaḷukkēṟṟa uṟiyuḷum uṇavum piṟa vacatikaḷiyum amittuk koṇṭu, tam āḷviṉit tiṟattāl ivvulakatti iṉpamē nukarum tēvarulakāka māṟṟiviṭṭaṉar.
tamiḻ vēntar tam nāṭṭiṉkaṇ vaḻippōvārkkuriya iṭiyūṟukaḷi vilakki, ellārum pōkku varavu puritaṟkuriya peruvaḻikaḷi amittu, avviṭaṅkaḷil vilaṅkukaḷālum kaḷvar mutaliya tīyavarkaḷālum tuṉpam nērātapaṭi paṭi maṟavarkaḷi niṟuvik kāttaṉar. palavūrkaḷukkuc cellum vaḻikaḷ oṉṟu kūṭi mayaṅkutaṟkuriya kavartta vaḻi, `kavali eṉappaṭum. ivvāṟu pala vaḻikaḷ kūṭiya neṟiyiṟcelvār tām cellum ūrukkuriya vaḻi iṉṉatu eṉat terintu koḷḷa iyalātu mayaṅkutaliyalpu. vaḻippōvār ivvāṟu mayaṅki iṭarppaṭuta lākāteṉa ṟeṇṇip paṇṭit tamiḻar pala vaḻikaḷ cantikkum iṭattilē tici kāṭṭum kalli niṟutti, akkallilē avvaḻi cellum ūrppeyariyum eḻutiyiruntaṉar. āṅkē palavūrkkuc celvārum ciṟitu nēram iḷippāṟutal iyalpātaliṉ, avar vaḻipaṭutaṟkuriya kaṭavuḷ ampalam amikkap peṟṟatu.
cellun tēettup peyarmaruṅku aṟimār
 kalleṟin teḻutiya nallari marāatta
 kaṭavu ḷōṅkiya kāṭēcu kavali.1
eṉap peruṅkuṉṟūrp peruṅkucikaṉār kūṟuvataṉāl ivvuṇmi teḷiyalām.
kaṭattaṟkariya pērāṟukaḷi eḷiyōrum kaṭantu cellutal karuti, ellāriṭamum kūli peṟātu aṟaṅkaruti nīrttuṟikaḷil  ōṭaṅkaḷ celuttap peṟṟaṉa. `aṟattuṟi ampi eṉap puṟanāṉūṟṟiṟ kuṟikkappaṭuvatu (puṟam. 381) ittaruma ōṭamēyākum.
oru nilattu makkaḷ, taṅkaḷ nilattiṟkiṭikkum poruḷ kaḷi maṟṟavariṭam koṭuttu, avarkaḷpāl uḷḷavaṟṟip paṇṭamāṟṟu muṟiyil vāṅkiṉārkaḷ.
iḷamparuvattu āṭavarkaḷum makaḷirum taṅkaḷ uṭal amitikkēṟṟapaṭi naṉṟāka viḷiyāṭiṉārkaḷ. iḷiñarkaḷ viḷiyāṭātu cōmpiyiruttal avarkaḷuṭiya uṭal vaḷarcciyiṉiyum uḷḷat tiṇmiyiṉiyum citikkum eṉap peṟṟōr aṟivuṟuttiṉar.
iṟantār uṭampiṉit tāḻiyiṟkavittup putittalum, viṟakiṭṭu erittalum ākiya iruvakip paḻakkaṅkaḷum tamiḻariṭiyē nilaviṉa. vīrarkaḷ pōrkkaḷattil uyir tuṟattaliyē taṅkaḷukkuriya ciṟappāka matittārkaḷ. aracar pōrkkaḷattil cāvāmal nōyiṉāl mutirntu iṟantaṉa rāyiṉum, avar uṭampiṉi vāḷāṟ piḷantu piṉṉarē aṭakkam ceyvatu vaḻakkam. itaṉāl tamiḻarkaḷ pōrt tuṟiyiṟ koṇṭa peruviruppamum avarkaḷuṭiya maṟa vuṇarcciyum naṉku pulaṉātal kāṇalām.
caṅkakālat tamiḻar aṟivu, āṇmi, poruḷ, paṭi eṉṉum nāṉku tiṟattilum vaṉmi peṟṟiruntaṉar; tamatu kalvittiṟattāl aṟivi vaḷarttaṉar; paṭittiṟattāl āṇmi peṟṟaṉar; uḻavu vāṇikam mutaliya toḻilkaḷāṟ poruḷ peṟum vaḻi tuṟikaḷip perukkiṉar; aṟivum āṇmiyum uṭiyārit tērnteṭuttu, avartam tuṇi peṟṟu nāṭṭiṉik kāttu, araciyali vaḷarttaṉar; naṭpiṉ tiṟattāl ulakiṉar ellāriyum tam cuṟṟamākak karuti, aṉpiṉi vaḷarttaṉar; aṟaṉāṟṟi mūtta aṟivuṭiyarāy, iṟivaṉi vaḻipaṭṭu, ellārkkum nallaṉavē ceytu makiḻntaṉar.
III

āṭaval nili
ettakiya ceyaliyum talimi tāṅkic ceytu muṭikkum iyalpu āṇmit taṉmiyākum. ivviyalpu āṭavar peṇṭir irupālāruḷ āṭavarukkē ciṟappāka uriyatām. toṭaṅkiya viṉikaḷi iṭiyē nekiḻaviṭātu ceytu muṭikkum viṉiyāṇmiyuṭiyār āṭavar eṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟṟaṉar. viṉittiṟattil veṟṟi peṟṟu viḷaṅkutal āṇmit taṉmiyi mēlum mēlum vaḷarppatākum. vāḻkkiyil nērum iṭiyūṟukaḷi etirttu niṉṟu veṟṟi kāṇutalē āṭavarkaḷiṉ kuṟikkōḷāka amital vēṇṭum.
valiya toḻilkaḷic ceytaṟkēṟṟa uṭalamippum uḷḷattiṇmiyum peṟṟavar āṭavarāvar. avarkaḷāṟ ceytaṟkuriya kaṭiya toḻilkaḷi melliyalārākiya peṇṭir ceytaṟkuriyarallar. tīṅkutarum uyirkaḷi nōkki añcāmi āṭavar iyalpu; añciyotuṅkutal peṇmiyiṉ iyal pākum. añcum iyalpuṭiya peṇṇiṉatti accamiṉṟik kāttaṟku utavuvatu āṇmiyiṉ āṟṟalēyākum. iv vāṟṟalāl maṉi vāḻkkikkuriya makaḷiri ātarittu, avarkaḷ accamiṉṟi vāḻat tuṇiceytal āṭavarkaḷiṉ kaṭamiyākak karutap peṟṟatu.
perumiyum uraṉum āṭavarkaḷiṉ paṇpukaḷām. oruvaṉ taṉ vāḻkkiyil ellārikkāṭṭilum taṉṉi uyar niliyil niṟuttal perumiyākum. kalvip payiṟciyiṉāl uṇṭākum nallaṟiviṉālum, ettakiya iṭiyūṟukaḷiyum añcātu etirttu niṟkum vīrattiṉālum, palarum taṉṉip pārāṭṭa vāḻum pukaḻiṉālum, tāṉīṭṭiya poruḷi iravalarkku variyātu vaḻaṅkum vaṇmiyiṉālum, oruvaṉ taṉ vāḻnāḷil ellāriyum viṭa mēṉmēl uyarntu viḷaṅkum perumiyiṉip peṟukiṉṟāṉ. ivvulakiyalil nērum palavaki iṭiyūṟukaḷiyum taṭuttu niṉṟu ulakil amiti nilavap pāṭupaṭum uṇarvumikka avaṉatu uḷḷat tiṇmiyē `uraṉ eṉak kuṟikkappaṭuvatām. tāṉ koṇṭa koḷkiyiṉi nekiḻa viṭāta uṟutiyum, maṉattiṉi naṉṉeṟik kaṇ niṟuttum kaṭṭuppāṭum, enta viṉiyiyum mayaṅkātu eṇṇit tuṇiyum tuṇipum, tiṇṇiya aṟivākiya uraṉuṭimiyiṉ tiṟaṅkaḷākum. maliyē vantu vīḻntālum nilikalaṅkāta uḷḷattiṇmi yuṭiyavaṉē `uravōṉ eṉap pōṟṟap peṟupavaṉāvaṉ.
uravōr vāḻum nāṭē urimi vāḻvu uṭiyatākum. tāṉ cemmiyāka vāḻtaṟkum, taṉṉāṭṭu araciyal cemmi yuṟutaṟkum uraṉuṭiyāḷaṉatu uḻippē iṉṟiyami yātatākum. caṅka kālattil vāḻnta āṭavarkaḷ araciyal vāḻvilum kuṭumpa vāḻvilum talivarkaḷāy niṉṟu viṉiceytaṟkuriya perumiyum uraṉum peṟṟiruntārkaḷ. makkaḷ piṟkālattil aṭiyum perumikaḷukkellām araṇceyvaṉa avarkaḷ iḷamiyiṟ paḻakum viḷiyāṭṭukka ḷēyām.
tamiḻiḷiñarkaḷ uṭalukkum uḷḷattiṟkum uṟutiyiṉi viḷikkum viḷiyāṭṭiṉi mēṟkoṇṭiruntārkaḷ. nīrniliyilum pērāṟukaḷilum kaṭalilum kutittu nīntutalum, viṟpōr maṟpōr mutaliya pōrt tuṟiyil payilutalum tamiḻiḷiñarkaḷiṉ paṇṭikkāla viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷāy amintaṉa. āḻamāṉa nīrniliyiṉ arukē ōṅki vaḷarnta marattiṉmēl ēṟiniṉṟu, kariyilē niṉṟavar viyappaṭiyumpaṭi annīr niliyilē tiṭīreṉak kutittu, ataṉ aṭiyilēyuḷḷa maṇṇik kiyāl aḷḷikkāṭṭi viḷiyāṭutal, tamiḻ iḷiñarkaḷiṉ viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷuḷ oṉṟākum. taḷarnta naṭiyiṉarāyt taṇṭūṉṟic cellum mutumip paruvattiṉa rākiya pulavar oruvar, kaṇṭār viyakkumpaṭi neṭunīrk kuṭṭattut tuṭumeṉap pāyntu ataṉ aṭiyiluḷḷa maṇṇik kiyāl eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya tam iḷamip paruvac ceyalip puṟanāṉūṟṟuc ceyyuḷ oṉṟil mikavum cuvipaṭa eṭuttuk kūṟi, ‘avviḷami ippoḻutu illi! eṉa iraṅkukiṉṟār.
`iḷamiyiṟ ciṟanta vaḷamiyilli, (naṟṟiṇi) eṉṟār oru pulavar. oruvaṉatu vaḷarccikkuriya paruvam iḷamiyē yātaliṉ, avviḷamip paruvatti oruvaṉ nilipeṟap pōṟṟikkoḷvatik kāṭṭilum avaṉ peṟutaṟkuriya celvam vēṟu oṉṟumilli eṉpatu appulavar karuttākum. iḷamip paruvattiṟkup poliviṉit taruvatu poruṭ celvamākum. poruḷillātavaṉatu iḷamip paruvam poli viḻanta niliyiṉatām. poruḷīṭṭutaṟkuriyār viṉiceytaṟ kaṇ ūkkamuṭiyavarāy iruppar. makkaḷāl poruḷeṉa matikkappaṭum. uṇmiyāṉa celvam uḷḷak kiḷarcciyākiya ūkkamuṭimiyēyākum. viṉiceytaṟkaṇ tōṉṟum uḷḷak kiḷarcci āṭavarkaḷukkuc ciṟappāka urittākum. viṉiceytalil uḷḷa ūkkam oruvaṉ uḷḷattu nili peṟṟirukkumāṉāl, avaṉukku uṇavu mutaliya vaḷaṅkaḷ kuṟintālum, avaṉ uṭampu tiṇmiyum aḻakum peṟṟut tikaḻum. viṉittiṟamākiya ūkkamuṭiyāṉoruvaṉ. aṟpa uṇavi uṇpāṉāyiṉum, avaṉuṭal tiṇmiyāṟ polivu peṟum eṉpar. eṉ talivaṉ pulliya uṇaviṉiyuṇṭum, maliyeṉa vaḷarnta periya tōḷkaḷi uṭiyavaṉāy viḷaṅkukiṉṟāṉ, eṉat talivi taṉ talivaṉip pārāṭṭu kiṉṟāṉ. ākavē, uṭampiṉ vaḷarccikku uṇavu mutaliya puṟac celvaṅkaḷiviṭa viṉi ceytaṟkaṇ tōṉṟum akamakiḻcci yākiya ūkkamē ciṟanta kāraṇamākum eṉpatu pulaṉām. viṉittiṟamē āṭavarkaḷukku uyirākum. `viṉiyē āṭavark kuyirē eṉṟār oru pulavar. uyirāṟṟalākiya ivviṉit tiṟattip peṟutaṟkēṟṟa vaṉmiyuṭiyatākat tammuṭampiṉi vaḷarttal tamiḻiḷiñar kaṭaṉāyiṟṟu.
tamiḻnāṭṭu iḷiñarkaḷ taṅkaḷ uyirvāḻkkiyiṉi oru poruḷāka matiyātu, ettakiya pakiyiṉiyum etirttu niṟkun tiṇmi peṟṟiruntārkaḷ. meliyāriṭattē avariṉum meṉmiyuṭiyarākap paṇin toḻukutalum, valiyāriṭattē avariṉum vaṉmiyuṭiyarākat talinimirntu naṭattalum āṭavarkkuriya talimip paṇpukaḷākum eṉat tamiḻmakkaḷ eṇṇiṉārkaḷ. tiṇmi illāta āṭavarkaḷ taṅkaḷ kuṭumpattik kāppāṟṟikkoḷḷum tiṟami peṟutaliyalātu. taṅkaḷiyē kāppāṟṟik koḷḷun tiṟami peṟāta ivarkaḷ, taṅkaḷ kuṭumpattiṉari evvāṟu pātukāttuk koḷḷa muṭiyum?
iṭiyūṟukaḷiyellām etirttu niṉṟu taṅkaḷ kuṭumpattik kākkum poṟuppu āṇ makkaḷukkē uriyatākum. tuṉpattāl taḷarntu cāyun taṅkaḷ kuṭiyiṉit taḷarāmal tāṅki niṟkum āṇmakkaḷ ovvoru kuṭumpattiṟkum iṉṟiyamiyātavarkaḷākak karutap paṭṭārkaḷ.
iḷiñarkaḷip pōrt tuṟiyil paḻakkum payiṟcik kūṭaṅkaḷ tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟ ciṟṟūrtōṟum niṟuvappeṟṟiruntaṉa. ūriluḷḷa iḷiñarkaḷiyellām oṉṟu cērttup pōriṟ paḻakkum appayiṟcik kūṭaṅkaḷ `pōravi eṉavum, `muraṇ kaḷari eṉavum vaḻaṅkap peṟṟaṉa. pōraviyiṟ payiṟci peṟṟa vīrarkaḷiṉ tiṟamiyiṉi uṇartal karuti āṇṭutōṟum ūrmakkaḷ pōrviḻā naṭatti vantārkaḷ. avviḻāviṟ kalantu koṇṭa iḷiñarkaḷ viṟpōrilum maṟpōrilum tāṅkaḷ peṟṟa tiṟamiyiṉip pōrviḷiyāṭṭiṟ pulappaṭuttiṉārkaḷ. iṅṅaṉam koṇṭāṭappaṭum pōrviḻā, `pūntoṭi viḻā eṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟṟatu. innāḷiṟ kallūriyiṟpayilum māṇavar kaḷukkup pōrppayiṟci tarutal iṉṟiyamiyātatu eṉa araciyalaṟiñarkaḷ pala muṟiyilum vaṟpuṟuttip pēcak kēṭkiṉṟōm. ippayiṟciyiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmiyi muṉṉarē uṇarnta tamiḻmakkaḷ, iḷiñarkaḷ kalvi payilum poḻutu avarkaḷukkup paruvamaṟintu pōrttoḻiliyum kaṟpittaṟkuriya vāyppiṉi aḷittārkaḷ.
cōḻarkuṭit tōṉṟalākiya perunaṟkiḷḷi eṉpāṉ, taṉṉāṭṭiluḷḷa iḷiñarkaḷukkup pōrp payiṟci tarutal karutip pōraviyiṉi niṟuvi, avvaviyil iḷiñarkaḷik kūṭṭip pōrttuṟiyiṟ paḻakkiṉāṉ. pōravikkut taliva ṉāy viḷaṅkiyatu karutip `pōravik kōpperu naṟkiḷḷi eṉṉum peyarāl avaṉ vaḻaṅkap peṟṟāṉ. avaṉāl naṭattappaṭṭa pōrppayiṟcik kūṭattil iḷiñarkaḷukku maṟpōrum viṟpōrum payiṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. aṅkup payilum iḷiñarkaḷ veḷiyūrkaḷukkuc ceṉṟu āṅkāṅkē payilum pōrvīrarkaḷuṭaṉ pōrttuṟiyil pōṭṭiyiṭuvataṉit taṅkaḷ viḷiyāṭṭākak koṇṭārkaḷ.
perunaṟkiḷḷi iḷiñaṉāy iruntapoḻutu cōḻa nāṭṭiṉ puṟattēyuḷḷa ōrūriṟ pōrviḻā nikaḻntatu. perunaṟkiḷḷi avviḻāviṟ kalantukoṇṭu taṉatu pōrt tiṟattip pulappaṭutti, makkaḷukku attuṟiyil ārva muṇṭākka eṇṇiṉāṉ; tāṉum pōr viḻāviṟ kalantu koḷḷa varuvatāka avvūr makkaḷukkaṟivittāṉ. ūrmakkaḷ avaṉatu varukiyip perum pēṟākak karuti, aṉpuṭaṉ varavēṟṟārkaḷ. avvūril `peruṅkōḻi nāykaṉ eṉṉum vaṇikaṉ makaḷārākiya `nakkaṇṇiyār eṉṉum iḷa naṅkiyār, kaṉṉimip paruvattilēyē tamiḻiṟ perumpulami peṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉār. nallicip pulami nirampiya annaṅkiyār, pōravik kōpperunaṟkiḷḷiyiṉ pērāṟṟalik kēḷviyuṟṟu avaṉpāl aṉpu mītūrap peṟṟavarātaliṉ, tammūril nikaḻum pōrviḻāvil tam vīṭṭiṉ muṉṟiliṉ orupāl otuṅki niṉṟu, viḷiyāṭa vanta perunaṟkiḷḷiyik kaṇṭu makiḻntār.
nakkaṇṇiyār vāḻum vīṭṭiṉ etirēyuḷḷa akaṉṟa maṇalveyilētāṉ pōrviḻā nikaḻtaṟkuriya ēṟpāṭukaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. uriya kālattē iḷiñarkaḷum potu makkaḷum tiraḷākak kūṭiyiruntārkaḷ. perunaṟkiḷḷiyiṉ tōḻarkaḷākiya vīrarkaḷ orupuṟamum avvūril vāḻum pōrvīrarkaḷ maṟṟoru puṟamum etir etirāka aṇivakuttu niṉṟārkaḷ. iru tiṟattu vīrarkaḷum tāṅkaḷ kaṟṟu valla pōrttiṟaṅkaḷi mēṉmēlum viḷaṅkak kāṭṭiṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷ ellārik kāṭṭilum perunaṟkiḷḷiyē taṉ pōrttoḻiṟṟiṟaṉi mikutippaṭutti viḷiyāṭiṉāṉ. ataṉik kaṇṭu makiḻnta cāṉṟōr, ‘perunaṟkiḷḷiyē veṉṟāṉ! eṉap peruka āravāram ceytaṉar. entavūrilum uṇmiyi maṟittuppēcum makkaḷ iruppatu iyalputāṉē? attakiya makkaḷ appōrviḻāk kūṭṭattilum iruntārkaḷ. veḷiyūriṉiṉṟu vanta perunaṟkiḷḷiyiṉ veṟṟiyi veḷiyiṭātu maṟittal vēṇṭumeṉpatu avarkaḷatu viruppam. avviruppattāl, `perunaṟkiḷḷi veṉṟāṉallaṉ eṉa aṉṉār āravārañ ceyvārāyiṉar. ivvāṟu oṉṟuk koṉṟu māṟupaṭa nikaḻum āravārattik kēṭṭa nakkaṇṇiyār, kālilaṇinta cilampukaḷārppa vīṭṭiṉiṉṟum veḷivantu, tam vīṭṭu mukappilē niṉṟa paṉiyiṉ tūrilē ēṟiniṉṟu, pōrviḻā nikaḻcciyi eṭṭip pārttār. tammāl aṉpu ceyyappaṭum peru naṟkiḷḷiyē veṉṟati nēriṟkaṇṭu teḷintār. im makiḻcciyiṉ viḷivākap pōravik kōpperunaṟ kiḷḷiyi avar viyantu pōṟṟiya pāṭaloṉṟu puṟanāṉūṟṟiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. (puṟam. 85).
kaṭaṟkariyilē vāḻum paratavarkaḷ taṅkaḷ uṭal valiyiṉip perukkikkoḷḷak karutip pōrppayiṟcikkuriya muraṇkaḷariyiṉi amittukkoṇṭārkaḷ. ataṉkaṇ paḻakiya vīrarkaḷ, maṇal paranta veḷiyilē niṉṟu, oruvark koruvar mutuku koṭāmal, kiyāṟ kuttiyum, paṭik kalaṅkaḷālē veṭṭiyum, oruvar uṭampuṭaṉ oruvaruṭampu tākkumpaṭi kalantu porutārkaḷ eṉṉum ceyti paṭṭiṉap pāliyiṟ kūṟap paṭṭuḷḷatu.
ivvāṟu iḷamip paruvattilēyē kalvippayiṟciyum pōrppayiṟciyum vāykkap peṟṟa kuṭumpamāṉatu ettakiya iṉṉalkaḷālum citiyātu vaḷarum ciṟappuṭiyatākum. kuṭumpattip pātukāttaṟkuriya nalla āṇmakkaḷip `poṉ pōṟputalvar eṉac cāṉṟōr pārāṭṭuvar. ataṉāl, putalvarkaḷip peṟātavar nāṭṭukkuc ceyyavēṇṭiya kaṭamiyiṉic ceyyātavarākavē karutappaṭṭaṉar. putalvarip peṟātavaruṭaṉ vīrarkaḷ pōrceyvatu kūṭāteṉpatu tamiḻ nāṭṭup pōraṟamākum. tam muṉṉōr toṭaṅkiya naṟceyki kaḷit tamakkup piṉṉum toṭarntu ceytaṟkuriyavarkaḷ tam putalvarkaḷum, avarkaḷ vaḻip piṟakkum pērar mutaliya varumāvar. āṇ makkaḷip peṟṟavarkaḷē taṅkaḷ muṉṉōrk kuriya viruppattiṉit toṭarntu niṟivēṟṟiyavarkaḷā vārkaḷ. putalvarp pēṟu vāykkap peṟātār, tamakkup piṉ muṉṉōr viṉikaḷit toṭarntu muṭittaṟkuriya cantati illāmiyāl, tam kaṭamiyiṉi niṟivēṟṟātavarākavē karutappaṭṭaṉar.
tam kuṭumpattiyum nāṭṭiyum kāttaṟporuṭṭut tammuyir koṭuttuc ceytaṟkuriya āṇmit tiṟattit tam vaḻiyiṉar peṟutal vēṇṭumeṉpatu tamiḻk kuṭiyiṟṟōṉṟi maṟinta muṉṉōrkaḷiṉ viruppamākum. muṉṉōr virumpiya ivviruppattiṉi niṟivēṟṟutal karuti āṭavarkaḷip peṟṟuk koṭuttal piṉṉuḷḷārkkuriya kaṭamiyāy amintatu (patiṟṟup pattu, 70).
tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ teṉpakuti kaṭalāṟ koḷḷap paṭṭami yāl, aṅkiruntu pukaḻuṭampiṉi niliniṟutti maṟinta tam muṉṉōrkaḷit teṉpulattār (teṉṟiciyil vāḻvār) eṉat tamiḻar vaḻipaṭuvārāyiṉar. `teṉpula vāḻnarkku aruṅkaṭaṉ iṟukkum poṉpōṟ putalvar 1 (puṟam. 9) eṉap pulavaroruvar āṇmakkaḷic ciṟappittup pārāṭṭukiṉṟār.
maṇañceytukoḷvataṟku muṟpaṭṭa paruvam kāḷip paruvam ākum. ivviḷamparuva vīrarkaḷ taṅkaḷ nāṭṭiṉ nalaṅkarutip pōrkkaḷattil pakivar paṭikaḷi etirttu niṉṟu, uyirvaḻaṅkum tiṟam peṟṟiruntārkaḷ. ivvāṟu āṇmi mikka vīrar palarum pōrttuṟiyil īṭupaṭṭu iṟantamiyāl, āḷviṉikkuriya āṭavar toki mikavum kuṟivatāyiṟṟu; tammittāmē kāttuk koḷḷutaṟkuriya uṭal vali peṟāta melliyalārākiya makaḷir toki mikuvatāyiṟṟu. ataṉāl, āṭavar peṇṭir ākiya iru pālāruḷ āṇ makkaḷip peṟutalē naṟpēṟākak karutum vaḻakkam nāṭṭil nili peṟuvatāyiṟṟu. maṇam ceytu koṇṭāruḷ putalvarp pēṟuṭiyār tam muṉṉōrkkuriya ariya kaṭamiyiṉic ceytu muṭittu, vāḻkki iṉpaṅkaḷi nukarntu mutirntavarātalāl, avarip pōriṟkollutal paḻiyuṭiya ceyalākak karutappaṭavilli.
kōpperuñcōḻaṉ uṇṇā nōṉpu mēṟkoṇṭu vaṭakkiruntu uyirviṭak karutiyapoḻutu, avaṉuṭiya uyir naṇparākiya pottiyār eṉṉum pulavar tam maṉivi piḷḷip pēṟaṭiyum niliyiliruntum tāmum avaṉuṭaṉ vaṭakkiruntu uyirviṭa muyaṉṟār. ataṉi uṇarnta kōpperuñcōḻaṉ pulavari nōkki, pukaḻcāl putalvaṉ piṟanta piṉ vā, eṉap paṇittup pottiyāri vīṭṭiṟku aṉuppi vittāṉ eṉpatum, aracaṉ paṇiyiṉi mēṟkoṇṭu vīṭu ceṉṟa pulavar tam maṉivi putalvaṉip peṟṟu uṭal nalam peṟṟa piṉṉart tirumpic ceṉṟu vaṭakkiruntu naṭukallākiya kōpperuñ cōḻaṉiyaṭintu, `iṭantaruka eṉa vēṇṭip peṟṟa, uṇṇātu vaṭakkiruntu uyir tuṟantār eṉpatum pottiyār pāṭiya puṟanāṉūṟṟup pāṭalāl iṉitu viḷaṅkum. ivvara lāṟṟic ciṟitu ūṉṟi ārāyntāl, tam nāṭṭil makkaḷ toki perukutal vēṇṭum eṉpatil tamiḻmaṉṉar pērārvam koṇṭiruntami naṉku pulaṉākum.
kaṭami
āṇmiyum āṟṟalum uṭiya putalvarkaḷip peṟṟu vaḷarttalit tāy taṉ kaṭamiyāka eṇṇiṉāḷ. taṉ putalvarkaḷukku niṟinta kalviyip payiṟṟi avikkaṇ ellāriṉum muntiyirukkac ceytaliyum avarkaḷip pōrttuṟiyil payiṟci nirampiya amiti uṭiya vīrarākkit taṉ nāṭṭiṟku uḻikkac ceytaliyum tanti taṉ kaṭamiyāka eṇṇiṉāṉ. pōrttuṟiyiṟciṟanta avvīrar pakivari veṉṟu mēmpaṭutaṟkuriya vēlum vāḷum mutaliya paṭik kalaṅkaḷiyum piṟavaṟṟiyum ceytukoṭuttu nāṭṭiṉ paṭittiṟaṉip perukkutaliyē kollar mutaliya toḻi lāḷar tam kaṭamiyākak koṇṭiruntaṉar. avvīrarkaḷukku araciyal vaḻiyoḻukum oḻuṅkumuṟiyiṉi aṟivuṟutti nilamum poruḷun tantu ātarittalit tamiḻ vēntar tammuṭiya kaṭamiyāka mēṟkoṇṭaṉar. vīravāḻvu vāḻtaṟkuriya cūḻniliyiṉiyuṭiya tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟ piṟantu vaḷarnta maṟavarkaḷ, pōrkkaḷattilē pakivar cēṉikaḷi aṟavē citittu, yāṉip paṭiyik koṉṟu, veṟṟiyuṭaṉ mēmpaṭutaliyē taṅkaḷ kaṭamiyākak karutiṉārkaḷ.
putalvarkaḷukkuriya kaṭamiyākac collappaṭṭa pōrt tuṟiyil tamiḻnāṭṭiṟpiṟanta ellā iḷiñarkaḷum kalantu payiṉṟārkaḷ. iḷiñarkaḷ taṅkaḷ nāṭṭiṉ nalaṅkarutip pakivari etirttal kaṭaṉ eṉpatu tamiḻmakkaḷāṟ karutap peṟṟatu. āṭavar ovvoru varum tattam iḷamip paruvattil pōrppayiṟci peṟṟut tamiḻppaṭi vīrarāy viḷaṅkiṉar. avar ellārum amitiyāṉa kālattil uḻavu mutaliya toḻilkaḷic ceytu vāḻkki naṭattuvar; pōrk kālaṅkaḷiṟ paṭiyiṟcērntu paṇiyāṟṟuvar; tam pōrt tiṟamiyoṉṟē karuti nāṭṭu makkaḷiṭam tavaṟāka naṭantu koḷḷāmal, yāvariṭattum paṇivāka naṭantu koḷvar.
pakivēntarip pōriṟ kollum pērāṟṟal peṟṟa vīraṉ oruvaṉ, pōrillāta amitik kālattil uḻutu payirceyyun toḻili mēṟkoṇṭiruntāṉ. ittoḻilic ceyyuṅkālattut taṉ kuṭumpattip pātukāttaṟkuriya uṇavillāmiyāl, taṉṉūr makkaḷiṭam uṇavukkuriya tāṉiyaṅkaḷik kaṭaṉākap peṟṟiruntāṉ. avaṉuṭiya varaku muṟṟi viḷintatu. varakiṉi aṟutta aritāḷ kuṟivāy iruntapaṭiyāl, erutukaḷikkoṇṭu, mitikkaviṭāmal, iḷiñarkaḷ taṅkaḷ kikaḷālē aritāḷkaḷi aṭittu varakiṉik kuvittārkaḷ. taṉ nilattil viḷinta varakiṟ perumpakutiyiṉi avvīraṉ taṉ kaṭaṉkārarkaḷukkuk koṭuttu viṭṭu, eñciyataṉip pacimikka pāṇarkaḷukkuk koṭuttaṉuppiṉāṉ; taṉ kuṭumpattār paciyiṉi nīkkutaṟkuc ciṟitu kūṭa viḷiporuḷ illāmiyāl, mīṇṭum piṟar pāṟceṉṟu varakiṉik kaṭaṉākap peṟa muyaṉṟāṉ. ivvāṟu ulaka naṭiyiṉi uṇarntu naṭantukoṇṭa tamiḻ maṟavaṉatu paṇpuṭimiyiṉip pulavaroruvar puṟap pāṭaloṉṟil (327) cuvi mika eṭutturikkiṉṟār. itaṉāl, aracari vellum peruvaṉmiyuṭiya vīrarkaḷum, taṅkaḷ maṉam pōṉapaṭi varampu kaṭantu oḻukāmal, ulakiyal varampukku uṭpaṭṭu, vāṅkiya kaṭaṉi oḻuṅkākat tiruppik koṭuttu, aṟattiṉ vaḻiyoḻu kiṉārkaḷ eṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
āṭavar tamakku etirillātapaṭi tamatu āṇmit taṉmiyi mēṉmēlum pōrkkaḷattiṟ pulappaṭuttutaliyē tammuṭiya poḻutupōkkākak koṇṭiruntaṉar. kāvaṟ peṇṭu eṉak kuṟikkap peṟṟa peṇpāṟ pulavaruṭiya makaṉoruvaṉ, peruvīraṉāy viḷaṅkiṉaṉ. avaṉ ep poḻutum pōrceytaliyē poḻutu pōkkākak koḷḷum āṇmi mikkavaṉāvaṉ. avaṉik kāṇac ceṉṟa naṇpaṉoruvaṉ, avaṉ tāyiṉip pārttu, ‘niṉ makaṉ eṅkēyuḷḷāṉ? eṉa viṉavi niṉṟāṉ. atukēṭṭa av vīraṉuṭiya tāy, avaṉi nōkki, niṉmakaṉ eṅkēyuḷḷāṉ eṉṟu eṉṉik kēṭkiṉṟāy. avaṉ evviṭattiruntālum avaṉi aṟiyēṉ. puli taṅkiyiruntu veḷiyē ceṉṟa kukiyiṉip pōla, avaṉip peṟṟa vayiṟō, ituvākum. avaṉ pōrkkaḷattiṉ kaṇṇē veṟṟiyāṟ polivu peṟṟut tōṉṟuvāṉ. aṅkē pōyp pārppāyāka, (puṟam. 86) eṉak kūṟiyaṉuppukiṉṟāḷ. ‘puliyiruntu pōṉamiyāl veṟuvitākiya kukikkum veḷicceṉṟa pulikkum yātoru toṭarpum illāmipōla, eṉakkum eṉ vayiṟṟiṟ piṟanta makaṉukkum ettakiya toṭarpum illi eṉak kāvaṟ
peṇṭu aṟivuṟuttiṉmi āṭavarkaḷatu vīra vāḻkkiyiṉ iyalpiṉiyum avarkaḷip peṟṟa tāyāriṉ vīrat tiṇmiyi iṉitu viḷakkuvatām.oru nāḷilē eṭṭut tērkaḷic ceyya valla taccaṉoruvaṉ oru mātam muyaṉṟu ceyta tērkkāl, virivum tiṇmiyum uṭiyatātalpōlap pōrt toḻilil virivum tiṇmiyum uṭiya vīrar palar ittamiḻakattilē vāḻntaṉar. tiṭīreṉap pōr ēṟpaṭumpoḻutu ūrmakkaḷ ellāriyum muracaṟintu aḻittaṟkāka ūr maṉṟattilē muracu toṅkaviṭappaṭṭiruttal marapu. ammuracu piṟarāl aṭikkappaṭātu peruṅkāṟṟiṉ mōtulāl ataṉkaṇ ciṟiya ōci tōṉṟumāṉālum, ataṉik kēṭṭup pōrp paṟiyeṉṟu karutip pōrukkup puṟappaṭum virivu uṇarcci tamiḻ vīrarkaḷpāl viḷaṅkiyatu. `ēṟu taḻuvutal eṉṉum muṟi akkāla āṇmakkaḷatu uṭal valiyiyum uḷḷat tiṇmiyi yum naṉku viḷakkuvatākum. iṅṅaṉam ceytaṟkariya vīrac ceyalkaḷic ceyyum āṟṟal peṟṟa āṭavar, tammi aṉpiṉāl kātalitta uḷamotta maṅkiyariyē maṇantu koṇṭaṉar. tammi virumpāta makaḷirik kūṭutal tammuṭiya āṇmit taṉmikku iḻukkākumeṉpatu avartam karuttu. taṉatu uṭal vaṉmi yoṉṟaṉiyē paṟṟukkōṭākak koṇṭu melliyalārākiya peṇṭiratu uḷḷattu uṇarcci yiṉi matiyātu uṇarvu kaṭantu oḻukupavaṉ āṭavarkkuriya uraṉ eṉṉum tiṇṇiya aṟiviṉip peṟātavaṉ eṉa ikaḻappaṭuvāṉ. virumpāta makaḷiri virumpi niṟpavaṉ uraṉillātavaṉ eṉa ellārālum ikaḻappaṭutal uṟuti. eṉṉi ikaḻnta aṟivillātavaṉi yāṉiyiṉ kālil akappaṭṭa mūṅkil muḷiyippōla varuntap porutilēṉā yiṉ, tītilāta neñcattāl kātal koḷḷāta makaḷiratu puṇarcciyiṭi eṉ māli tuvaḷvatāka! eṉac cōḻaṉ nalaṅkiḷḷi vañciṉam kūṟukiṉṟāṉ. viruppamillāta makaḷirik kūṭutali nallāṇmimikka āṭavar aruvaruppākak karutuvar eṉpatu mēṟkūṟiya nalaṅkiḷḷiyiṉ vañciṉattāl naṉku pulaṉām.
taṉṉi aṉpiṉāl kātalitta melliyalāḷākiya makaḷiṭattu avaḷikkāṭṭilum meṉmiyāḷaṉāyp paṇintoḻukutalum, vīrattiṉ vaṉmiyāl taṉṉi yotta āṭavarkaḷiṭattil avarkaḷik kāṭṭilum pērāṇmi paṭittavaṉāy niṉṟu avarkaḷi aṭakki āḷutalum nallāṇmi mikka vīraṉukkuriya paṇpukaḷākap paṇṭit tamiḻarkaḷ viḷakkiyuḷḷārkaḷ. āṭavar tamakkotta aṉpuṭiya makaḷiri nāṭit tirumaṇam ceytukoṇṭa piṉṉar, maṉiyil iruntu illaṟam nikaḻttum urimiyiṉi vāḻkkit tuṇiviyarākiya maṉiviyarkku vaḻaṅkiṉar.
maṉiviyuṭaṉ makiḻntu illiruntu nallaṟam ceytaṟ kēṟṟa poruḷi īṭṭutal āṭavarkaḷiṉ kaṭamiyākum. vaṟumiyiṉ koṭumiyāl paci nīṅka vāḻa muṭiyāta iravalar kaḷukku vēṇṭum poruḷik koṭuttu ātarittu aṟam ceytalum, tamakkaṭaṅkāta pakivari veṉṟaṭakkutalum, viyattuḷ vāḻvāṅku vāḻntu iṉpam nukartalum ākiya ulakiyalil nikaḻum ellāc ceyalkaḷum poruḷāl nikaḻtaṟkuriyaṉavē. `poruḷillārkku ivvulakamilli, eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar. ākavē, tam vāḻkkikku vēṇṭum poruḷit tēṭutal āṭavariṉ kaṭamiyāyiṟṟu. taṉ muyaṟciyāl vanta poruḷik koṇṭē oruvaṉ taṉ vāḻkkiyiṉi nikaḻttutal vēṇṭum eṉpatu tamiḻar koḷki. oru toḻilum ceyyātu taṉ muṉṉōr tēṭi vitta poruḷik koṇṭu āravārattuṭaṉ vāḻpavaṉ uyiruṭiyavaṉākak karutappaṭuvatilli. maṇaṅkoḷḷumuṉ peṟṟōratu ātaraviṉkīḻ vāḻnta, makaṉ, uriya paruvam vantatum taṉ maṉattiṟkiṉiya maṅkiyi maṇantu vāḻa virumpukiṉṟāṉ. avaṉ tāṉ virumpiya maṅkiyi maṇam ceytukoḷḷutaṟkuriya poruḷip peṟṟōriṭam vēṇṭip peṟuvatilli. taṉatu tirumaṇatti muṉṉiṭṭup poruḷīṭṭak karutiya avaṉ vēṟṟu nāṭu celvatu vaḻakkam. itaṉi `variviṭi vittupa poruḷ vayiṟpirital (paṇatti otti vittu viṭṭu) eṉpar. talivaṉ maṇantu koṇṭa piṉṉarum poruḷīṭṭutaṟkeṉa maṉiviyip pirintu cellutal uṇṭu. `irappārkku illiyeṉṟu colvativiṭa iṟattalē mēl, eṉpatu tamiḻarkaḷatu eṇṇamākum. aṟampuri neñcattavarāyt tamiḻ nāṭṭu āṭavar vāḻntaṉar. maṉikkaṇ maṉiviyuṭaṉiruntu vāḻvataṉ nōkkam, viruntiṉarkkaḷittu, vaṟumiyāḷar ellārkkum nallāṟṟiṉ niṉṟa tuṇiyāy utavi ceytalē eṉpatu avartam koḷkiyāy amintatu. āṇmiyuṭiyār vīṭukaḷ, iravalarkkum piṟarkkum aṭiyā vāyiliyuṭi yaṉavāy viḷaṅkiṉa. maṉiviyuṭaṉ vāḻkki naṭattuṅkāl āṭavar tam nāṭṭiṟku varum iṭiyūṟukaḷi nīkkutal karuti, aracaṉukkut tuṇiyāyp pōr ceyyap pirintu cellutal uṇṭu.
`pōrkkaḷattilē vīrattiṟkut tāṉē elliyāy viḷaṅkum vīraṉoruvaṉ, maṉikku viḷakkantarum taṉ maṉivi yoṭu kūṭi vāḻntu, nāṭṭuk kuṭikaḷuḷ oruvaṉāy viḷaṅku kiṉṟāṉ. avaṉē pakivar taṉ nāṭṭiṟ paṭiyoṭu pukuntu tīṅku ceytaviṭattu, avartam cēṉi veḷḷattit taṭukkum aṇiyākavum viḷaṅkukiṉṟāṉ, eṉap pulavar oruvar tamiḻnāṭṭu āṭavaratu iyalpiṉip puṟappāṭal oṉṟil (314) eṭutturikkiṉṟār. itaṉāl, tamiḻ nāṭṭiluḷḷa āṭavarkaḷ innāṭṭiṉ kuṭi makkaḷāy viḷaṅkiyatuṭaṉ, camayam nērnta poḻutu pōr ceytu nāṭṭiṉik kākka valla paṭi vīrar kaḷākavum toṇṭu ceytārkaḷ eṉpatu naṉku teḷiyappaṭum.
āṇ makkaḷ tāṅkaḷ vāḻum nilap pakutiyi ellāp poruḷkaḷum viḷivataṟkuriya niliyil uḻavu mutaliya toḻilkaḷic ceytu vaḷamuṭiyatākkiṉārkaḷ. avar kaḷuṭiya iṭiviṭāta uḻippiṉāl nīrillāta pāliyum vaḷamuṭiyatāy māṟiṟṟu. ellā vaḷaṅkaḷum oruṅku aminta nāṭāyiṉum, an nilattil vāḻum āṭavarkaḷ uḻippiṉṟic cōmpi iruppārkaḷāṉāl, annāṭu oru payaṉum tarutalilli. ākavē, nilattip paṟṟiya muṟiyil ataṉi vaḷamuṭiya tākavum vaḷamaṟṟatākavum pirittup pēcutal poruntā teṉavum, nalla niṉivum nalla uḻippumuṭiya āṭavarkaḷ enta nilattil vāḻkiṉṟārkaḷō, anta nilamē naṟpayaṉ tarumeṉavum paṇṭit tamiḻc cāṉṟōr uyttuṇarntaṉar. nilamāṉatu, nīr vaḷamuṭiya nāṭu, marañceṟinta kāṭu, mēṭu, paḷḷam eṉap palvēṟu iyalpukaḷi uṭiyatātal karuti annilap pakutiyiṉi `naṉṉilam eṉap pārāṭṭiyum, `puṉṉilam eṉap paḻittum potu makkaḷ pēcuvārkaḷ. vaḷamillāta nilamāyiruntālum, nalla niṉivum uḻippum uṭiya āṭavarkaḷ vāḻntāl, nalla payaṉkaḷit tarumeṉavum, vaḷamārnta naṉṉilamāyiṉum, tīya niṉivum cōmpalumuṭiya tīyavar vāḻntāl, oru ciṟitum payaṉpaṭāteṉavum uyttuṇarnta auviyār, nilamē, nī nāṭāy iruntālum, kāṭāy iruntālum, mēṭāy iruntālum, paḷḷamāy iruntālum, niṉakku eṉa naṉmi tīmiyiṉi uṭiyi alli. niṉpāl vāḻum āṭavarkaḷ eṅkeṅkē nalla eṇṇamum uḻippum uṭiyavarkaḷāy viḷaṅkukiṉṟārkaḷō, avvavviṭaṅkaḷil nīyum nalla payaṉit tarukiṉṟāy. ātalāl, nī iṉitu vāḻvāyāka! eṉa oru puṟappāṭalāl (187) nilatti vāḻttukiṉṟār. ivvāḻttu āṭavarkaḷiṉ naṉmuyaṟciyāl nila iyalpu vaḷam peṟum uṇmiyiṉi naṉku viḷakkutal kāṇalām.
nalla vaḷamuṭiya nāṭāyiṉum, maṉanalamillātu aṟamallātaṉa ceyyum āṭavarkaḷip peṟṟirukkumāṉāl, atu taṉ vaḷam keṭṭuc citiyumeṉpar. āṟṟilē nīnti viḷiyāṭiya iḷanaṅki orutti ki taḷarntu veḷḷattiṉ vaḻiyē cella, atu kaṇṭa iḷiñaṉ oruvaṉ tuṭumeṉa āṟṟiṟkutittu avaḷik kariyēṟṟik kāppāṟṟiṉāṉ. taṉṉik kāppāṟṟiya āṭavaṉiyē tāṉ maṇantukoḷḷa vēṇṭumeṉpatu annaṅkiyiṉ viruppam. avviruppatti uṇarntukoḷḷāta tantiyum tamiyaṉmārum avaḷi vēṟoruvarkku maṇañ ceyyak karutiṉar. talimakaḷatu aṉpukku māṟāka aṟam allaṉa ceyyat tuṇiyum avarkaḷ ceyal nāṭṭiṉ iyaṟki vaḷattic citittuviṭum eṉa uṇarnta tōḻi, immaliyil vāḻvār tam makaḷi aṉpiṉāl kāppāṟṟiya talivaṉukkuk koṭukka niṉiyātu ayalāṉ oruvaṉukkuk koṭukka niṉintu aṟattukku māṟāka naṭantukoḷḷuvatāl, iṉi immali nilattil vaḷḷik kiḻaṅkum naṉṟāka viḷiyā; malimēl tēṉaṭikaḷum toṭukkappaṭamāṭṭā; puṉaṅkaḷil tiṉikaḷum niṟinta katirkaḷi īṉāvām! eṉac cevilit tāyiṭam kūṟi varuntu kiṉṟāḷ.1 nilattil vāḻum nalla āṭavarkaḷiṉ niṉivum ceyalum paṟṟiyē annilam vaḷam peṟutal kūṭumeṉap paṇṭit tamiḻ makkaḷ eṇṇiṉami tōḻiyiṉ kūṟṟāl naṉku pulaṉām.
evvaḻi nallavar āṭavar
 avvaḻi nalli vāḻiya nilaṉē!


IV
peṇṭir nili
aḻakiṉālum, aṉpu aruḷ mutaliya paṇpiṉālum virumpat takka taṉmi peṇmiyākum. āṭavarkaḷāl virumpip pōṟṟat takka aṉpu, aṭakkam, amiti mutaliya naṟpaṇpukaḷum, appaṇpukaḷukkēṟṟa uṭal vaṉappum uṭiyavarē peṇṭir eṉap pōṟṟap peṟuvar. kaṇṇiṟinta pēraḻakiṉāl uḷaṅka varum naṅkiyik `kāriki eṉa vaḻaṅkutal ulakiyal. āṭavar kaḷāl kātalikkat takunta kātal mikka makaḷir `mātar eṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟṟaṉar. aimpoṟikaḷāl nukartaṟkiṉiya meṉṉīrmi makaḷiriṉ ciṟappiyalpākum. makaḷirpāṟ kāṇappeṟum meṉṉīrmiyiṉic `cāyal eṉpar. cāyalākiya meṉmi yiṉit tam iyalpākap peṟṟamiyāl, `melliyalār eṉṟa peyar makaḷirkku uriyatāyiṟṟu.
āṭavar accamiṉṟi nāṭeṅkuñ cuṟṟit tirintu iyaṟkiyāluḷavākum iṭiyūṟakaḷi etirttu niṉṟu viṉi ceytal pōlap puṟattoḻiliṟ kalantu koḷḷum viruppam peṇṭirkku iyalpaṉṟām. makaḷiratu uṭalamippu valiya toḻilkaḷic ceytaṟku ēṟṟataṉṟu. uṭaliṉ tiṇmi peṟāta melliyalār vilaṅku mutaliyavaṟṟāl ulakiyalil nikaḻum iṭiyūṟukaḷi etirttu niṟṟaṟkup pōtiya āṟṟaluṭi yārallar. tuṉpantarum iṭar neṟikaḷiṟcellātu otuṅki niṉṟu tam uḷḷattu amitiyāl tammittāmē pātukāttuk koḷḷum āṟṟal peṇṭiriṉ tiṟamiyākum.
accam, nāṇam, maṭam eṉpaṉa peṇṭirkkuriya kuṇaṅkaḷām. makaḷiratu uḷḷattil kuṟippiṉṟit tōṉṟum naṭukkam accamākum. tam peṇmit taṉmikkup poruntāta puṟac ceyalkaḷil otuṅki oḻukutal nāṇamākum. ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷiṉ iyalpiṉip piṟar aṟivikka aṟintu tam aṟivāl mēṟkoṇṭa koḷkiyiṉi nekiḻa viṭātu pōṟṟutal maṭaṉākum. peṇkaḷukku iyalpāka aminta ikkuṇaṅkaḷ avarkaḷ vāḻkkiyil mēṉmēlum uṟutipeṟṟu vaḷarvaṉavām. taṉṉi maṇantukoṇṭa talivaṉukku ettakiya tīṅkum nērtalākātu eṉat talimakaḷiṭattē tōṉṟiya accam, avaḷ taṉkaṇavaṉiṭattē vitta aṉpu kāraṇamākap piṟantatākum. aṟivum āṇmiyum cālpum peṟṟa āṭavaṉoruvaṉit taṉ āruyirt talivaṉākak karuti maṇantukoḷḷa virumpiya vaḻi, avaṉkaṇ uḷḷam oṉṟi aṭaṅki yoḻukutal, kāmak kuṟippiṉāl tōṉṟiya nāṇamākum. avaṉoruvaṉiyē vaḻipaṭutal vēṇṭum eṉat tāṉkoṇṭa koḷkiyi nekiḻātu kaṭippiṭittal kaṟpākum. taṉ kaṇavaṉē teyvameṉak karuti maṉañcaliyātu oḻukum uḷḷat tiṇmiyē kaṟpeṉappaṭum. taṉakkuc ciṟantāṉ oruvaṉiyē taṉ uyirt tuṇivaṉākak kaṭippiṭittoḻukum uṟuti yuṭiyavaḷē `orumi makaḷ eṉap pōṟṟap peṟuvaḷ. ayalāṉoruvaṉatu viruppiṟkiṇaṅkit taṉ uḷḷat tuṟutiyiṉi nekiḻa viṭa niṉittal kalakkamākum. iṅṅaṉam māṟupaṭṭuk kalaṅkatal uḷḷattiṇmiyillāta iḻinta makaḷiriṉ ceyalākum. kalaṅkā niliyākiya kaṟpeṉṉum tiṇmi yuṭiya makaḷirē peṇṭir eṉa matittup pōṟṟutaṟ kuriyavarāvar.
tam peṇmit taṉmiyiṉ iyalpiṉi aḻiyātu kāttuk koḷḷutalum, tam kaṇavarip pēṇi avar colvaḻi aṭaṅkiyoḻukutalum, tam iruvariṭattum amitaṟkuriya naṉmi niṟinta pukaḻiṉi nīṅkāmaṟ pātukāttalum peṇṭirk kuriya kaṭamikaḷām.
tamiḻ makkaḷ vīratti viḷittaṟkeṉa āṇpiḷḷi kaḷip peṟa virumpiyatu pōlavē, aṉpum aruḷum ākiya maṉap paṇpiṉi vaḷarttaṟkuriya peṇmakkaḷiyum peṟa virumpiṉārkaḷ. aṉpum aṟivum aminta paṇpuṭiya peṇmakkaḷip peṟutali virumpiya makkaḷ, attakiya naṉmakkaṭpēṟṟiṉi aruḷutal vēṇṭum eṉa iṟivaṉi vaṇaṅkip pōṟṟiṉārkaḷ. icceyti,
kuṉṟak kuṟavaṉ kaṭavuṭ pēṇi
irantaṉaṉ peṟṟa elvaḷik kuṟumakaḷ 1
eṉa varum kapilar pāṭalāl iṉitu pulaṉām.
tam uṭalamippiṟkum uḷḷa iyalpukkum ēṟṟa viḷiyāṭalkaḷic caṅkakālat tamiḻ makaḷir mēṟkoṇ ṭiruntaṉar. maṇaliṟ ciṟuvīṭu kaṭṭutal, kaḻaṟcikkāy ammaṉik kāy mutaliyavaṟṟik kiyāṟpiṭittu āṭutal, paṟavi uyarntu paṟakkumāṟu pōla unti paṟattal, pantāṭutal, ūcalil ēṟiyāṭutal, pūkkoytal, puṉalviḷiyāṭal mutaliyaṉa makaḷiriṉ iḷamparuva viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷām. mēṟkuṟitta viḷiyāṭalkaḷ yāvum makaḷirkku uṭalil tiṇmiyiṉiyum uḷḷattu eḻucciyiṉiyum tantu, avartam uṭal nalattiṉi naṉku pātukāppaṉavām. ivviḷiyāṭalkaḷiṉ iṉṟiyami yāmiyiṉi uṇarnta peṟṟōr tam makaḷiri naṉṟāka viḷiyāṭi makiḻumpaṭi vaṟpuṟuttiṉar. viḷiyāṭum makaḷir kūṭṭam `āyam eṉṉum collāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭatu. `vīṭṭiṉ puṟattē pōntu viḷiyāṭum paruvattu iḷamakaḷir, tam tōḻiyarkaḷuṭaṉ kūṭi viḷiyāṭi makiḻātu, vīṭṭiṉ kaṇṇē aṭippaṭṭiruttal aṟaṉum ākātu; avarkaḷ uṭaliṉ ākkattiyum citippatākum, eṉa aṟiñar palarum aṟivuṟut tiṉar. ataṉāl,  iḷamakaḷir, tam tōḻiyarkaḷuṭaṉ kūṭi viḷiyāṭi makiḻātu, vīṭṭiṉ kaṇṇē aṭipaṭṭiruttal aṟaṉum ākātu; avarkaḷ uṭaliṉ ākkattiyum citip patākum, eṉa aṟiñar palarum aṟivuṟuttiṉar. ataṉāl, iḷamakaḷir ellārum  putuppuṉalāṭiyum, pūkkoytum, māli mutaliyaṉa toṭuttaṇintum viḷiyāṭi makiḻntaṉar.
vilaṅkiṉiṉṟum makkaḷi vēṟu pirittu uyarttuvatu kalvi. attakiya kalviyi āṭavar peṇṭir ākiya iru pālarum payilutal vēṇṭum eṉpatu paṇṭit tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ koḷkiyākum. uruvum, tiruvum, aṟivum mutaliyavaṟṟāl tammōṭu otta takuti vāynta makaḷiriyē tamiḻiḷiñar maṇantu koṇṭaṉar. peṇṭir kalvi payilutaṟku uriyarallar eṉṉum piḻipaṭṭa koḷki tamiḻaṟiñarkku uṭaṉpāṭaṉṟām. aṭakkamum, amitiyum, maṉaṅkōṭāmiyum, naṉṟum tītum pakuttuṇarum nallaṟivum, ārāyntuṇartaṟku ariya arumip paṇpum peṇṭirkkuriya ciṟappiyalpukaḷākumeṉap paṇṭit tamiḻāciriyar variyaṟuttuk kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. 1 ippaṇpukaḷ yāvum, niṟinta kalvip payiṟciyuṭiya caṉṟōrkaṇallatu ēṉiya potu makkaḷiṭattē kāṇappaṭāta arumiyuṭiyaṉa vākum. ākavē, mēṟkuṟitta arumip paṇpukaḷukkellām nilikkaḷamāy viḷaṅkutaṟkuriya peṇṭir, āṭavarkaḷip pōṉṟu niṟinta kalviyuṭiyarātal vēṇṭumeṉpatu tamiḻ muṉṉōr koḷkiyātal naṉku viḷaṅkum.
āṭavar peṇṭir ākiya iru pālarkkum kalvi potuvāyiṉum, āṭavar kalvi payilum muṟi vēṟākavum, peṇṭir kalvi payilum muṟi vēṟākavum muṉṉiyōr vakuttiruntaṉar. poruḷīṭṭalum, pōr ceytalum, nāṭu kāttalum mutaliya puṟattuṟikkuriya muṟiyil āṭavar kalvi payiṉṟaṉar. maṉi vāḻkkiyiṉi naṭattalum, aṉpiṉāl maṉa amitiyi vaḷarttalum ākiya kuṭumpap paṇiyiṉi nikaḻttaṟku ēṟṟa muṟiyil makaḷir tam vīṭṭiliruntē kalvi payiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ payiṉṟa kalvi vāḻvāṅku vāḻum payaṉuḷḷa kalviyāy amintiruntatu. ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷiṉ iyalpiṉiyum uyirkaḷiṉ uḷḷattuṇarccikaḷiyum uḷḷavāṟu uyttuṇarum nuṇṇaṟivu peṇkaḷiṉ taṉiyurimiyākum.
caṅkakālat tamiḻ makaḷir, iyal ici nāṭakam eṉṟa muttamiḻt tuṟiyilum ciṟanta pulami peṟṟut tikaḻntaṉar. iḷam paruvattilēyē uyarnta pulami peṟṟu viḷaṅkiya nallicip pulami melliyalār palar, paṇṭināḷil vāḻntaṉar. avarkaḷuṭiya aṟivurikaḷic cevimaṭutta tamiḻ vēntar kaḷum potumakkaḷum avarkaḷ aṟivuṟuttiya naṉṉeṟiyilē aṭaṅkiyoḻukiṉārkaḷ. ippoḻutu namakkuk kiṭittuḷḷa caṅkattoki nūlkaḷil aimpatiṉmarkkuk kuṟiyāta peṇ pāṟpulavarkaḷ pāṭiya ceyyuṭkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kākki pāṭiṉiyār eṉṉum peyaruṭiya peṇpāṟ pulavar iruvar tamiḻukkuc ciṟanta yāppilakkaṇa nūlkaḷi iyaṟṟiyuḷḷaṉar. 2
icippayiṟci makaḷirkku iṉṟiyamiyātatākak karutap peṟṟatu. ici pāṭutaṟku iṉiya kural paṭittavar peṇṭirē yāvar. icittamiḻi vaḷarttalil āṭavarik kāṭṭilum peṇṭirē mutaliṭam peṟṟaṉar. yāḻ icittal, kuḻalūtutal, mirutaṅkam vācittal mutalākak karuviyāṟceyyum icit toḻililēyē āṭavar payiṟci peṟṟiruntaṉar. āṭavariṉ miṭaṟṟōciyikkāṭṭilum, makaḷiratu kuralē cevikku iṉimi taruvatākum. ātalāl, kaṇṭattāṟpāṭun toḻiliṟ ciṟanta payiṟciyiṉip peṟutal makaḷirkku vāyppuṭiya ceyalāyiṟṟu. icittamiḻil valla makaḷir, tam iciyiṉ iṉimiyiṉāl yāṉi mutaliya valiya vilaṅkukaḷiyum aṭakkiyāḷum tiṟam peṟṟiruntaṉar.
malivāṇar makaḷir, tiṉippuṉattilē taṅkik kiḷi mutaliyavi tiṉiyi uṇṇātapaṭi oṭṭuvatu vaḻakkam. iravup poḻutilē paraṇmītamarntu puṉam kākkuṅ kāṉa vaṉoruvaṉ, kaḷḷuṇṭa kaḷippāl mayaṅki uṟaṅkiṉāṉ. anniliyilē iḷaṅkaḷiṟoṉṟu tiṉippuṉattiṟ pukuntatu. ataṉiyuṇarnta avaṉ maṉiviyākiya koṭicci taṉ maṇaṅkamaḻuṅ kūntalik kōtiniṉṟu iravilē pāṭutaṟku ēṟṟa kuṟiñcip paṇṇiṉi mikavum iṉimiyākap pāṭiṉār. amiḻteṉa iṉikkum avviṉṉiciyiṉic cevi maṭutta iḷaṅkaḷiṟu, tāṉ virumpi vanta tiṉik katiriyum uṇṇāmal, tiṉip puṉattiṉi viṭṭut tirumpic cellutaliyum niṉiyāmal, eppoḻutum eḷitil mūṭappeṟāta taṉ kaṇkaḷ mūṭutalip peṟṟu, niṉṟa niliyiṉiṉṟum peyarāmal uṟaṅkiyatu, eṉṟa ceyti akanāṉūṟṟup pāṭaloṉṟiṟ kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. ikkuṟippiṉi uṟṟu nōkkuṅkāl, paṇṭit tamiḻ makaḷir pāṭiya ici, koṭiya vilaṅku kaḷiyum amitiyuṟac ceyyun tiṟamuṭiya teṉpatu naṉku teḷiyappaṭum. pōriṟpuṇpaṭṭa tam kaṇavaratu nōyiṉit tām payiṉṟa iṉṉiciyākiya maruntiṉāl makaḷir taṇivitta ceyti puṟappāṭaloṉṟiṟ (281) kūṟappaṭṭatu.
caṅkakālat tamiḻ makaḷir, nāṭakat tamiḻilum nalla payiṟci peṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉar. uḷḷak kuṟippiṉit tam uṭampiṟ ṟōṉṟum meyppāṭukaḷāl pulappaṭuttum muṟi nāṭakattiṉ pāṟpaṭṭatākum. immuṟiyiṉi `viṟal eṉṟa collāṟ kuṟippiṭuvar. viṟalpaṭa āṭun tiṟam makaḷirkkē uriyatākum. viṟalpaṭa āṭa valla vaḷ `viṟali eṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟṟāḷ.
uḷḷattiṟku uvakiyaḷikkum nuṭpat toḻilkaḷik kaliyeṉṟa collāl vaḻaṅkutal marapu. kaviṉ kalikaḷip payilutaṟkuc ciṟappurimiyuṭiyār makaḷirēyāvar. āṭalum pāṭalum aḻakum amintu, tām kaṟṟu valla kalittiṟattāl maṉi vāḻkkiyic cemmippaṭutti makiḻvaḷikkum tiṟaṉ makaḷiratu matinalamākum. yāḻum kuḻalum ākiya icik karuvikaḷi vācittal, panteṟi kaḷattil niṉṟu tiṟam peṟap pantāṭutal, umiḻtaṉiya uṇavamikkum camiyaṟṟoḻiliṟ paḻakutal, uṭampiṟ pūcutaṟkuriya naṟumaṇap poṭiyiṉi amittal, paruvanilikkup porunta uṇavu mutaliyavaṟṟi amit toḻukutal, piṟar uḷḷak karuttiṉik kuṟippiṉālaṟiyum tiṟam peṟal, tām eṇṇiya karuttukkaḷi moḻinaṭiyiṟ piḻiyiṉṟit tokuttuk kūṟum col vaṉmi peṟutal, kaṇkaḷik kavarum ōviyam varital, malar māli toṭuttal, kaṇkum cōtiṭamum mutaliya nūṟṟurikaḷi aṟital eṉpaṉa makaḷir ciṟappākap payilutaṟkuriya kali vikaṟpaṅkaḷām. vāḻkkiyil makiḻcci viḷikkum kalikaḷ palavaṟṟiyum tamiḻ makaḷir aintām vayatu nirampip paṉṉiraṇṭām vayatu muṭiyavuḷḷa ēḻāṇṭukaḷilum naṉṟākak kaṟṟuṇarntaṉar.
kali vaḷam peṟṟa mātarkaḷ, tāṅkaḷ iṭiviṭātu pōṟṟutaṟkuriya nāṇamum kaṟpum aṉpum aruḷum mutaliya naṟpaṇpukaḷit taṅkaḷ uḷḷattil nilipeṟa vaḷarttum, taṅkaḷi aṇukātu taṭuttaṟkuriya kuṟṟaṅkaḷi aṟavē vilakkiyum, taṅkaḷit tāṅkaḷē niṟiyiṉāṟ kāval ceytu oḻukiṉārkaḷ; ariya viṉikaḷic ceytu muṭittaṟkēṟṟa viṉittiṟamum, aṉpum, aṟivum, vīramum mutalākiya naṟpaṇpukaḷum nal loḻukkamum vāynta āṇ makaṉiyē maṇantukoṇṭārkaḷ. mulli nilattil vāḻum āyarkaḷ taṅkaḷāl vaḷarkkap peṟṟa valiya erutukaḷip piṭittu aṭakkum āṟṟaluṭiyāṉukkē taṅkaḷ makaḷiri maṇañceytu koṭuttārkaḷ. `kollun toḻiliyuṭiya kāḷikaḷiṉ kompiṉ koṭumiyiṉi niṉintu añcum iyalpuṭiyāṉi āyar kulap peṇ maṟupiṟappilum maṇantukoḷḷa virumpamāṭṭāḷ,1 eṉac cōḻaṉ nalluruttiraṉār eṉṉum pulavar perumāṉ kūṟum moḻi akkālat tamiḻ makaḷiriṉ viruppattiṉi naṉku veḷippaṭuttuvatākum. makaḷiri maṇantukoḷpavar am makaḷir aṇitaṟkuriya aṇikalaṉkaḷukkeṉap perumporuḷip paricamākak koṭuppatu akkāla vaḻakkamākum. paricap poruḷi virumpit takutiyillātāṉi maṇantukoḷḷum vaḻakkattiṉit tamiḻ makaḷir veṟuttu vilakkiṉar. `naṉmi aminta perum poruḷip paricamākak koṭuppiṉum, uyarnta takuti yillātāṉi eṉ makaḷ maṇantukoḷḷa māṭṭāḷ, eṉṟu collit tantiyoruvaṉ peṇ koṭukka maṟutta ceyti puṟanāṉūṟṟup pāṭaloṉṟiṟ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. 1
kaṇavaṉatu vāḻkkikkut tuṇiyāy niṉṟu aṉpiṉāl oṉṟi vāḻum makaḷir, vāḻkkit tuṇi yeṉap pārāṭṭap peṟṟaṉar. maṉiyiṉkaṇ iruntu maṉiyaṟam nikaḻttum urimi makaḷirkkē vaḻaṅkap peṟṟatu. ataṉāl, `maṉivi, illāḷ eṉṟa peyarkaḷ peṇkaḷukku uriyavāyiṉa. ivvāṟu maṉiyiṉ urimiyiṉik kuṟittaṟkuriya peyarkaḷ āṇ pālārkku vaḻaṅkāmiyāl, maṉiyaṟak kaṭamikaḷil makaḷirē poṟuppuṭiyarāy viḷaṅkiṉar eṉpati aṟiyalām. palvēṟu toḻilkaḷiṟ karuttuṭiyarāy iṭiviṭātu viṉi ceytu uḻalum āṭavarkaḷi viṉi muṭiviṉkaṇ vīṭṭil amitiyākat taṅkiyirukkac ceytu maṉi vāḻkkikku viḷakkam taruvār makaḷirēyāvar. ivvāṟu maṉi vāḻkkiyiṟ poliviṉit tarum maṉiviyi maṉikku viḷakkākiya vāṇutal 2 eṉap pulavaroruvar pārāṭṭip pōṟṟukiṉṟār.
taṉṉāl toḻappaṭum teyvam vēṟu, taṉ kaṇavaṉ vēṟu eṉṟu karutāmal, kaṇavaṉiyē vaḻipaṭuteyvamākak karuti, aṉpiṉāl aṭaṅkiyoḻukutal paṇṭit tamiḻ makaḷiriṉ paṇpākum. uṟakkattiṉi viṭṭu eḻutaṟkuriya viṭiyaṟkāliyil teḷinta cintiyuṭaṉ vaḻipaṭu kaṭavuḷi vaṇaṅkiyeḻuvatu ulakiyal, `teyvatti mutaṟkaṇ toḻutu niṉṟu eḻātu, taṉ kaṇavaṉiyē mutaṟkaṇ vaṇaṅki niṉṟu tuyileḻuvāḷ, `pey eṉṟu coṉṉa aḷavilē maḻi peyyum, eṉṟār teyvap pulavar.
teyvan toḻāaḷ koḻunaṟ ṟoḻuvāḷ
 peyyeṉap peyyum maḻi.

eṉpatu tirukkuṟaḷ (55). iṅṅaṉam kūṟutalāl, peṇkaḷ kaṭavuḷi vaṇaṅkak kūṭāteṉpatu āciriyar tiruvaḷḷu vaṉāruṭiya karutteṉat tavaṟāka eṇṇiviṭutalākātu; kālip poḻutil eḻuntu kaṭavuḷi vaḻipaṭutaṟku muṉ tamakkuk kaṭpulaṉākun teyvamākiya kaṇavaṉi mutaṟkaṇ vaḻipaṭutal vēṇṭumeṉpatē āciriyar karuttākum.
tammāṟ kātalikkap peṟṟa talivaṉiyē maṇantu koḷḷa vēṇṭumeṉṟa viruppattāl makaḷir iṟivaṉi malar tūvi vaḻipaṭutal marapākum. `malivāṇar makaḷorutti, taṉṉāl virumpappaṭṭa talivaṉukkun taṉakkum virivil maṇam nikaḻa vēṇṭumeṉṟa pērārvattāl, tam kulamutaṟ kaṭavuḷāy maliyil eḻuntaruḷiyuḷḷa iṟivaṉi naṉṉīruṭaṉ naṟumalarkaḷik kiyiṟkoṇṭu aruccittu vaḻipaṭṭaṉaḷ, eṉa aiṅkuṟu nūṟṟup pāṭa loṉṟiṟ kapilar (259) kūṟukiṉṟār.
`civaperumāṉ, māyōṉ, murukaṉ mutalāya teyvaṅ kaḷukku vaḻipāṭu ceytaṟkeṉa antiyiṟ ceyyat toṭaṅkiya tiruviḻāvilē maturi nakarattil vāḻum pēriḷampeṇṭir tāmarip pūviṉik kiyilē piṭittāṟpōṉṟu tām peṟṟa iḷaṅkuḻantikaḷik kiyiṉāl taḻuvikkoṇṭu, tam kaṇavaruṭaṉ pūcikku vēṇṭum pūvum naṟumpukiyumākiya poruḷkaḷōṭu tirukkōyilukkuc ceṉṟu, iṟivaṉi vaḻipaṭṭaṉar, eṉa māṅkuṭi marutaṉār kūṟukiṉṟār.1 tirupparaṅkuṉṟatti aṭinta makaḷir murukap perumāṉatu vaḻipāṭṭiṟ kalantu koṇṭa iyalpiṉic caṅka nūlākiya paripāṭal iṉitu viḷakkukiṉṟatu. ‘yām em kātalarik kaṉavilē maṇantatu poyyākāmal, naṉaviṉkaṇ em tirumaṇattiṉi niṟivēṟṟiyaruḷuka! eṉa maṇamākāta makaḷir murukaṉi vēṇṭik koṇṭaṉar. maṇañceytukoṇṭa makaḷir, em vayiṟu piḷḷippēṟu vāyppatākuka!’ eṉavum, ‘em kaṇavar ceyyum ceyalkaḷ naṉku niṟivēṟuka!’ eṉavum, ‘em kaṇavar pōril veṟṟi peṟuvārāka! eṉavum vēṇṭi niṉṟu murukap perumāṉi aṉpuṭaṉ vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. maṇantukoṇṭa peṇṭir tam kaṇavaratu iṉitu makiḻcciyuṭaṉ kūṭiya pēraṉpiṉip peṟutal karutiyum, maṇamākāta kaṉṉiyar aḷivu tiru āṟṟalkaḷāṟ kuṟivaṟṟa mintari maṇantu koḷḷutal karutiyum ivvaḻipāṭṭiṟ kalantukoṇṭaṉar eṉap paripāṭaliṟ pulavaroruvar kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār.mārkaḻi mātattut tiruvātiri nāḷil  civaperumāṉukkut tiruviḻā naṭipeṟṟateṉavum, avviḻā muṭinta piṉṉar maturi nakarattu iḷamakaḷir, ‘ivvulakam maḻiyāṟ kuḷirvatāka! eṉat tāyarukkē niṉṟu kaṭavuḷi vaṇaṅkit tinnīrāṭiṉar eṉavum āciriyar nallantuvaṉār paripāṭaliṟ kūṟukiṉṟār. it tin nīrāṭal makaḷir mēṟkoḷḷutaṟkuriya tavac ceyalkaḷuḷ oṉṟākak kalittokiyiṟ kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟatu.1 ‘maṉiviyuṭaṉum tāy tantiyaruṭaṉum piḷḷikaḷuṭaṉum cuṟṟattāruṭaṉuṅkūṭit teyvatti vaḻipaṭa vēṇṭum, eṉap periyōr aṟivuṟuttiyapaṭi caṅka kālat tamiḻ makkaḷ kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭṭu muṟiyi amittuk koṇṭārkaḷ. avvaḻi pāṭṭil nampikkiyum uṟutiyumuṭiyavarkaḷāy muṉṉiṉṟu tam kaṇavarkkum putalvar mutaliyavarkkum iṉṟiyamiyāta naṟporuḷkaḷi vēṇṭip peṟutal peṇṭiratu ceyalāka amivatāyiṟṟu.

kaṇavar ulakiyaṟkaṭami nōkkit tammip pirintu ceṉṟa kālattu, avaruṭiya piriviṟku varuntātu kuṟitta nāḷaḷavum āṟṟiyiruntu maṉiyaṟam nikaḻttutal peṇṇiyalpu eṉap pārāṭṭappaṭuvatām. tam kaṇavariyē uyirākak karutum orumi makaḷir, niṟiyiṉ vaḻuvāmal tammit tāmē kāttuk koḷḷum uṟuti yuṭiyārāvar. ittakiya uṟutiyāl uḷḷat tiṇmi peṟṟa kaṟpuṭi naṅkiyi maṉiviyākap peṟutalik kāṭṭilum oruvaṉ aṭitaṟkuriya ciṟanta pēṟu vēṟoṉṟumilliyeṉa āciriyar tiruvaḷḷuvar vaṟpuṟuttik kūṟukiṉṟār. 2
kaṇavaṉi uṇavu mutaliyavaṟṟāl pēṇik kāttalum, avaṉatu varuvāykkut takkapaṭi āravāramaṟṟa niliyiṟ kuṭumpac celaviṉi amittuk koḷḷutalum, peṟṟatu koṇṭu uḷḷantiruntum amitiyiṉi uṭiyaḷātalum, periyār kaḷiyum viruntiṉarkaḷiyum pēṇip pōṟṟutalum, taṉ aṟiviṉ tiṟattiyum āṟṟaliyum veḷippaṭuttik koḷḷāmal amintu oḻukutalum, taṉ kuṭumpattiṟkup pukaḻuṇṭākkutalum maṉiviyiṉ māṇpukaḷeṉap pārāṭṭapṭum naṟkuṇa naṟ ceykikaḷākum.
‘piṟanta vīṭṭiliruntu nukarum iṉiya tēṉ kalanta pālikkāṭṭilum, eṉ talivaṉuṭaṉ ceṉṟa kālattup parukiya māṉ uṇṭu eñciya kalaṅkaṟ ciṉṉīr eṉakkup peritum cuviyuṭiyatāyiṟṟu! eṉat tamiḻ naṅki yorutti kūṟukiṉṟāḷ.1 kaṇavaṉ kuṭumpamō, vaṟumi niliyaṭintatu. peṇṇiṉ tantiyō, mikap periya celvamuṭiyavaṉ. tanti taṉ makaḷ vīṭṭiṟku vēṇṭiya uṇavu mutaliya poruḷkaḷi niṟiyak koṭuttaṉuppiṉāṉ. tantiyiṉ poruḷip peṟṟut tām iṉitāka vāḻtal takutiyaṉṟu eṉavuṇarnta talivi apporuḷik koḷḷātu, taṉ kaṇavaṉ īṭṭiya ciṟu poruḷi vittukakoṇṭu oru poḻutuviṭṭu oru poḻutu uṇṭu taṉ kuṭumpattip pōṟṟi varum cemmi peṟṟāḷ, eṉṟa arumiyāṉa nikaḻcci naṟṟiṇip pāṭaliṟ (110) kuṟikkap peṟukiṉṟatu. tam kuṭumpattiṉ vaṟumiyiṉit tantikkum pulappaṭuttik koḷḷāta cemmaṉac celviyarāyt tamiḻ makaḷir vāḻntami mēṟkāṭṭiya kuṟippiṉāl naṉku pulaṉām. tēvarula kattil nukarap peṟum iṉpattiviṭat tam kaṇavaṉuṭaṉiruntu nukaruntuṉpam mikavum iṉiyateṉpatu tamiḻ makaḷir karuttu. kuṭumpa vāḻkkiyil uṇṭākun tuṉpaṅkaḷiyellām poṟuttukkoṇṭu tam kaṭamiyic ceyyun tiṟam tamiḻ makaḷiriṉ taṉic ciṟappākum.
tammuṭiya celvamaṉiyi nīṅki veḷiyē naṭantaṟiyāta pēreḻil vāḻkkiyiṉarākiya kaṇṇakiyār, tam kaṇavaṉākiya kōvalaṉ ‘maturikkup puṟappaṭuka, eṉṟa aḷavil uṭaṉē puṟappaṭṭuk kālkoppuḷaṅkoḷḷum paṭiyākak kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattiliruntu maturikku naṭantu ceṉṟa varalāṟu, kēṭpōratu uḷḷattiyurukkum taṉmiyiṉatākum. kaṇṇaki yāruṭiya cīṟaṭikaḷiṉ meṉmiyiṉiyaṟintu avartam meṉmik kēṟpa nilamakaḷ nekiḻntu koṭukkavilliyēyeṉa avaruṭaṉ ceṉṟa kavuntiyaṭikaḷ iraṅkukiṉṟār. veyiliṉ vemmiyāl tam kaṇavaṉatu uṭalvāṭiyatē eṉṟu naṭukkamuṟṟu varuntiya niliyil tam tuyarattiṉi oru ciṟitum uḷaṅ koḷḷāta perumi kaṇṇakiyāriṭam amintiruntatu. ‘ivvāṟu iṉiya vāḻkkit tuṇiyākiya makaḷirkku iṉṟiyamiyāta kaṟpu māṇpiṉiyuṭiya kaṇṇakiyākiya itteyvamallatu itaṉiṉum ciṟanta aḻakumikka teyvatti yām kaṇṭilēm! eṉak kavuntiyaṭikaḷākiya tavac celviyār kaṇṇakiyārip pukaḻntu pōṟṟukiṉṟār. ‘pattiṉip peṇṭir irunta nāṭu kāla maḻi poyttaṟiyātu; viḷivu kuṟintu vaḷam piḻittali aṟiyātu; vēntaratu veṟṟi citintaṟiyātu, eṉa nallār palarum pārāṭṭiyataṟkēṟpac ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ eṉṉum cēramaṉṉaṉ pattiṉitteyvamākiya kaṇṇakiyārkkuk kōyileṭuttu vaḻipāṭu nikaḻttiya ceyaliṉiyum ataṉi uṇarnta ēṉiya tamiḻ vēntarkaḷum ilaṅki vēntaṉākiya kayavākuvum taṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ nāṭṭiṟ pattiṉit teyvamākiya kaṇṇakiyāri vaḻipaṭṭa iyalpiṉiyum cēramuṉivarākiya iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ cilappatikārattil ciṟappākap pārāṭṭiṉami ivaṇ karutaṟkuriyatām.
amiḻtiṉum iṉiya uṇaviṉic cuvi mikac camittut tam kaṇavaṉi uṇpittaliṟ pērārvamuṭiyarātal peṇṭiriṉ iyalpākum. tāṉ aṉpiṉāṟ camitta nalluṇaviṉi mikavum iṉiyatu eṉac collit taṉ kaṇavaṉ uṇṇutalik kaṇṭu makiḻtalikkāṭṭilum maṉiviyorutti peṟutaṟkuriya ciṟanta makiḻcci vēṟoṉṟumilli. talimakaḷ orutti cuvi mikka puḷik kuḻampiṉi amittut taṉ kaṇavaṉi uṇpitta tiṟattiṉik kuṟuntokip pāṭaloṉṟiṟ (167) pulavar nayam peṟa viḷakkukiṉṟār. talivaṉum taliviyum maṉiyaṟam nikaḻttum vīṭṭiṟkuc ceṉṟu vanta cevilittāy, talimakaḷatu kuṭumpa vāḻkkiyiṉ ciṟappiṉi naṟṟāykkuk kūṟuvatāka amintatu acceyyuḷ. `aṭukkaḷiyil aluval mikutiyāl nekiḻnta āṭiyiṉit tayir picinta kiyiṉāl virintu iṟukavuṭuttukkoṇṭu, tāḷippiṉ naṟumpuki taṉ kaṇṇiṟ paṭintu maṇaṅkamaḻ naṉṟākat tuḻāvic camitta puḷikkuḻam piṉit taṉ kaṇavaṉ mikavum cuviyuṭiyateṉṟu virumpi uṇṇutalik kaṇṭu taliviyiṉ mukam makiḻccik kuṟippuṭaṉ viḷaṅkiyatu, eṉpatu acceyyuḷiṉ poruḷākum.
tamiḻnāṭṭu makaḷir viruntiṉari upacarittalil mikavum tiṟamuṭiyavarāvar. tammiṭam uḷḷa poruḷ mikak kuṟi vāyiṉum, vanta viruntiṉar palar eṉṟu karutāmal, ellāriyum iṉmukattuṭaṉ uṇpikkum iyalpu avarkaḷpāl amin tiruntatu; ataṉāl, kuṟinta celavilē niṟinta payaṉkaḷi uṇṭākkum āṟṟalpeṟṟavarkaḷāy viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ. uṇmik kātal vayappaṭṭa makaḷir, tam kaṇavar tammuṭaṉ aḷavaḷāvu talil tavirntoḻukuvarāyiṉum, avarkaḷiṭattut tām koṇṭa aṉpil oru ciṟitum kuṟintoḻukuvārallar. ciṟupiḷḷikaḷ tāṅkaḷ viḷiyāṭutaṟkuriya ciṟu vaṇṭiyiṉi ēṟic celutta muṭiyāviṭṭālum, iḻuttu naṭantu iṉpuṟutal pōla, eṉ kaṇavari muyaṅki iṉpuṟēṉāyiṉum, avarkkuriya aṭittoṇṭukaḷic ceytu iṉpuṟṟēṉ, eṉat talivi yorutti kūṟukiṉṟāḷ.1
pakivar muṉṉē āṇ ciṅkattiṉip pōlat tali nimirntu naṭakkum āṇmiyiṉit tam kaṇavarkku aḷikka vallavar kaṟpuṭip peṇṭirēyāvar. ivvāṟu tām pukunta kuṭumpattiṟku ākkan tarutalpōlat tām piṟanta kuṭi yiluḷḷārkku veṟṟiyum pukaḻum viḷivippārum ammakaḷirē eṉpatu aṟiñar koḷki. malivāṇar makaḷir tam kaṇavarit teyvameṉṟu vaṇaṅki eḻutalit tamatu kaṭamiyākak koṇṭamiyāl, avaruṭaṉ piṟanta āṭavar tām toṭutta ampukaḷi ilakkut tappāmal eyyum āṟṟal peṟṟaṉar, eṉak kapilar kuṟiñcikkaliyiṟ (39) kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār.
tamiḻ makaḷir tam kaṇavaṉip piriyātu. vāḻum peṟṟi yiṉarāvar. tam āruyirt talivaṉ iṟakka nērntāl, neñcu kalaṅki, avaṉatu pirivāṟṟātu uṭaṉuyir viṭum peruṅkēṇmi centamiḻp peṇkaḷiṉ ciṟanta paṇpākum. āriyappaṭi kaṭanta neṭuñceḻiyaṉ taṉatu araciyal piḻittamikku iraṅki uyir tuṟantapoḻutu avaṉ maṉiviyākiya kōpperuntēvi taṉṉuyir koṇṭu avaṉuyirit tēṭic celvāḷ pōlat tāṉ uṭaṉē uyir tuṟantami tamiḻ nāṭṭup peṇkaḷiṉ taliyāya kaṟpiṉi iṉitu pulappaṭuttu vatām. kaṇavaṉ iṟantamikku iraṅkiya makaḷir tī vaḷarttu ataṉkaṇ vīḻntu iṟattalum uṇṭu. pūtap pāṇṭiyaṉ iṟanta poḻutu avaṉ tēviyākiya peruṅkōp peṇṭu, cāṉṟōr palar vilakkavum kēḷātu, tīppāyntu uyir nītta ceyti itaṉi valiyuṟuttum.
kaṇavaṉ iṟantamiyāl putalvar mutaliya kuṭumpattavar kaḷi vaḷarkkuṅ kaṭamiyiṉi mēṟkoṇṭa makaḷir, oru nāḷikku oru vēḷiyē uṇavaruntik kimmi nōṉpiṉi mēṟkoṇṭaṉar. kaṇavaṉiyiḻanta niliyil ātaravaṟṟa immakaḷir, piṟaruṭiya utaviyiṉi etirpārātu paruttip pañciṉi nūlāka nūṟṟut tam kuṭumpattip pātukāttaṉar. ivarkaḷ tam kuṭumpa varuvāykkuriya toḻilāka nūl nūṟṟali mēṟkoṇṭamiyāl, `paruttip peṇṭir eṉṟa peyarāl tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷiṟ kuṟikkap peṟṟaṉar.
kuḻantikaḷi vaḷarttal tāyiṉ kaṭamiyākum. taṉ piḷḷikaḷ nalla uṭal tiṇmiyum uḷḷattiṇmiyum peṟṟuc cāṉṟōrāka viḷaṅka vēṇṭumeṉpatē tāyiṉ peruviruppākum. piḷḷikaḷiṉ iḷamparuvattilēyē avarkaḷ uḷḷattil nalla paṇpukaḷi vaḷarttalil tamiḻt tāyar karuttuṭiyarāyiruntaṉar; tam uyiraṉiya tamiḻ moḻiyiṉit tam piḷḷikaḷukkut tiruttamuṟak kaṟpittaṉar; teruvil naṭi paḻakum kuḻavip paruvattilēyē tam piḷḷikaḷukkuc centamiḻ naṭiyiṉit teḷivākak kaṟpittaṉar; tām kaṟpitta coṟkaḷ cilavaṟṟi maḻali nāviṉāṟ kuḻantikaḷ kūṟak kēṭṭup peritum iṉpuṟṟaṉar; aṟattiṟ pōr ceytu veṟṟi peṟutalum, tammiṭamuḷḷa poruḷi illātārkku variyātu vaḻaṅkutalum ākiya naṟceyalkaḷiyē virumpi mēṟkoḷḷum paṭi tammintarkaḷukku aṟivuṟuttiṉar; kuḻantikaḷiṉ uḷḷam tīcceyaliṟ cellātapaṭi taṭuttuk kāttaṉar. ellāp paṇpukaḷum nirampap peṟṟuc cālpuṭiya peru vīrarkaḷāy viḷaṅkutaṟkuriya nalluṇarcciyiṉit tamiḻ makkaḷukku ūṭṭiya perumi tamiḻ nāṭṭup peṇpālārkkē ciṟappāka uriyatākum.
vīrakkuṭiyiṟ piṟanta makaḷir `mūtiṉ makaḷir eṉap pōṟṟap peṟuvar. ivartam iyalpiṉip puṟanāṉūṟṟuc ceyyuḷāl naṉkuṇaralām.
‘narampukaḷ eḻuntu taciyularnta uṭampiṉi uṭiya mutumakaḷorutti, taṉ makaṉ pōrkkaḷattil mutukiṟ puṇpaṭṭut tōṟṟāṉ. eṉṟu cilar tavaṟākak kūṟiyataṉik kēṭṭāḷ. ‘pōrilē eṉ makaṉ puṟamutukiṭṭiruppāṉāṉāl, avaṉukkup pālūṭṭiya eṉ mārpiṉi aṟuttiṭuvēṉ! eṉac ciṉaṅkoṇṭu pōrk kaḷattiṟkuc ceṉṟu aṅkuḷḷa piṇakkuviyalil taṉ makaṉ uṭampit tēṭiṉāḷ. mārpiṟ puṇpaṭṭuc citinta makaṉuṭampik kaṇṭu, avaṉē cāṉṟōṉ eṉavuṇarntu, avaṉip peṟṟa poḻutik kāṭṭilum perumakiḻcci aṭintāḷ, eṉṟu kākkipāṭiṉiyār nacceḷḷiyār eṉṉum pulavar kūṟukiṉṟār.

maṟakkuṭiyiṟpiṟanta maṟṟoru peṇ, mutal nāṭpōril taṉ tamiyaṉum, aṭutta nāṭpōril taṉ kaṇavaṉum iṟanta niliyil taṉ kuṭikku oruvaṉāyuḷḷa iḷañciṟuvaṉi vēl kikkoṭuttup pōrkkaḷattiṟ kaṉuppiṉāḷ eṉa okkūr mācāttiyār kūṟukiṉṟār. mūtiṉmakaḷirākiya ivarkaḷuṭiya vīrac ceyalkaḷ tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ maṟavuṇarcciyiṉi veḷip paṭuttuvaṉavām.
nāṭṭil aṭikkaṭi pōr nikaḻntamiyāl, āṭavar toki curuṅki makaḷir eṇṇikki perukuvatāyiṟṟu. ataṉāl, maṇantukoḷḷum vāyppillāta makaḷir āṭalum pāṭalum  aḻakum eṉṟa mūṉṟiṉum kuṟiviṉṟi māntar maṉattip piṇikkum ici nāṭakam ōviyam mutaliya aḻakuk kalikaḷiṟ poḻutu pōkkuvārāyiṉar. uḷḷattiṟku uvakiyaḷikkum aṟupattu nāṉku kalikaḷiyum naṉku kaṟṟut tuṟi pōkiya ivarkaḷ, oruvaṉukkē urimi pūṇṭoḻukum tiṟamiṉṟip potu makkaḷ ellāriyum iṉpuṟuttum aḻakuk kalikaḷi vaḷarttu vantārkaḷ; ātalāl, eṇṇeṉ kaliyōr 1 eṉap pārāṭṭap peṟṟārkaḷ. oruvaṉukkē uriyarāy vāḻum niyatiyiṉṟi, ayalārākap paḻakutaliṉ, ivarkaḷ `parattiyar eṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ oruvaṉukkē urimi pūṇṭu maṉiyaṟak kaṭamikaḷi naṉku nikaḻtti vāḻtalum uṇṭu. kōvalaṉuṭiya kātaṟkiḻatti mātaviyiṉ niṟiyuṭimi ivaṇ niṉikkat takuvatām.
V
tamiḻar kalvi nili
kalviyeṉpatu makkaḷ aṟiya vēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟi aṟitaṟ kuriya maṉap payiṟciyākum. uṭkaruviyākiya maṉamum kaṇ mutaliya puṟak karuvikaḷum oṉṟi niṉṟu ivvulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷi nōkkiyuṇarum ippayiṟci muṟiyiṉālēyē makkaḷatu aṟivu vaḷarcciyaṭikiṉṟatu.
kalvi vaḷarccikku moḻiyiṉ tuṇiyē iṉṟiyamiyāta tākum. makkaḷ tāṅkaḷ eṇṇiya karuttukkaḷi maṟṟavarkaḷukku viḷaṅkac collutaṟkum, piṟar eṇṇaṅkaḷit tāṅkaḷ kēṭṭaṟitaṟkum avarkaḷāṟ pēcappaṭum tāymoḻiyē iṭiniṉṟu tuṇi ceyvatām. ākavē, tamiḻ muṉṉōr kaḷāl kalviyeṉak kuṟikkappaṭṭatu, avartam tāymoḻi vaḻiyākap payilum tamiḻk kalviyē eṉpatu pulaṉām.
makkaḷuṭiya pulamic celvattiṉ kuṉṟāka nilikkaḷa māy viḷaṅkuvatu avarkaḷ pēcum moḻiyēyākum. ataṉ utaviyiṉālēyē avartam kalvi uruvākiṉṟatu. moḻiyiṉ tuṇiyiṉṟik kalviyi vaḷarttal eṉpatu iyalāta ceyalām. ivvuṇmiyi naṉkuṇarnta tamiḻ muṉṉōr, tam tāy moḻiyākiya tamiḻik kalvip payiṟcikkuriya tiṟamuṭiyatāka amittuk koṇṭaṉar.
ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷiṉ iyalpiṉi uḷḷavāṟu viḷakkutaṟkuriya collamippiṉiyuṭiyatu `iyaṟṟamiḻ eṉappaṭum. makkaḷ maṉattil amintu kiṭanta eṇṇaṅkaḷi uruvākkic ceyaṟpaṭuttaṟkuriya iyalpiṉi veḷippaṭuttum tiṟam iyaṟṟamiḻukkuriyatākum. tām collak karutiya karuttukkaḷik kēṭpār uḷḷam makiḻa iṉiya ōciyōṭu kūṭiya icit tiṟattāṟ pulappaṭuttum moḻinaṭi `icit tamiḻ ākum. taṉ eṇṇaṅkaḷ tam uṭampiṟ kāṇappaṭum meyppāṭu mutaliyavaṟṟāl veḷippaṭṭut tōṉṟa naṭittuk kāṭṭutaṟku aminta moḻi naṭi, `nāṭakat tamiḻ eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭum. epporuḷiyum teḷivāka eṇṇiyaṟiyum uḷḷattiṉ iyalpiṉi vaḷarttaṟkuriya moḻiyamippiṉi iyaṟṟamiḻ eṉṟum, kēṭpār uḷḷattiṉik kuḷirac ceyyum iṉṉōci mikka uriyiṉ iyalpiṉi icit tamiḻ eṉṟum, makkaḷ colvaṉa avarkaḷ uṭaliṟṟōṉṟum meyppāṭukaḷāl pulappaṭum muṟiyil aminta moḻi naṭiyiṉi nāṭakat tamiḻ eṉṟum mikap paḻiya kālattilēyē nam tamiḻ muṉṉōr vakuttuḷḷaṉar. makkaḷ taṅkaḷ uṭampiṉ ceyalāl viḷaṅkik koṇṭavaṟṟi uriyiṉāl aṟivuṟuttavum, uriyiṉāl aṟivittavaṟṟi uḷḷattāl uyttuṇaravum tuṇi ceyyum karuviyākat tamiḻ moḻi amintamiyāl, ataṉi `muttamiḻ eṉṟa peyarāl muṉṉiyōr kuṟippiṭu vārāyiṉar.
makkaḷ taṅkaḷ maṉap payiṟciyiṉ payaṉāka uṇarntu koṇṭa iyalpukaḷi ōr oḻuṅku muṟiyilē vittu ēṉiyōrkku eṭutturippatē kalvip payiṟcikkuriya nūlākum. aṟiya vēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟi aṟivikkum karuvi, `nūl eṉappaṭum. nuṇṇiya pañciṉ nuṉikaḷāl ōriḻippaṭutti nūṟkum nūlip pōṉṟu, āḻntakaṉṟa poruḷuṭiya coṟkaḷāl muṟippaṭa iyaṟṟappaṭṭatē `nūl eṉac ciṟappikkappaṭum. muṟippaṭa iyaṟṟap peṟṟami karuti ataṉi `muṟi eṉṟa peyarāl vaḻaṅkutalum uṇṭu.
kalvip payiṟcikkuriya nūliṉi `eṇ eṉavum `eḻuttu eṉavum iru pakutiyākap paṇṭit tamiḻ makkaḷ vakip paṭuttiṉārkaḷ. kālam, iṭam, poruḷ eṉpavaṟṟiṉ aḷaviṉi uḷḷattāl eṇṇik kūṟupaṭutti aṟitaṟkuriya payiṟciyiṉi vaḷarkkuṅ karuvi `eṇṇūl eṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟum. eṇ eṉṉum col ikkālattil `kaṇitam eṉṟa poruḷil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. mikap periya aṇṭaṅkaḷiyum mikac ciṟiya aṇuviṉiyum eṇṇiyaṟitaṟkuriya pēreṇkaḷiyum ciṟṟeṇ kaḷiyum tamiḻ muṉṉōr muṉṉarē kaṇṭaṟintiruntaṉar.
aṇṭam mutaliya poruḷkaḷiṉ akalam, nīḷam, niṟi mutaliyavaṟṟi aḷantu kūṟutaṟkuriya kōṭi, caṅkam, tāmari, neytal, veḷḷam mutaliya pēreṇkaḷum, oru poruḷiyē paṉṉūṟu kūṟākap pakuttuṇartaṟkuriya kāṇi, muntiri mutaliya ciṟṟeṇkaḷum tamiḻ makkaḷāl muṉṉarē iyaṟṟap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. kaṇakkiṉik `karuvi `ceyki eṉa iruvakiyākap pirittu viḷakkiya ērampam mutaliya tamiḻk kaṇakku nūlkaḷ tam kālattiliruntaṉa veṉap parimēlaḻakar kūṟukiṉṟār. cintikkum moḻikkum appāṟpaṭṭa mutaṟkaṭavuḷākiya iṟivaṉ oruvaṉit tavira, ēṉiya ellāp poruḷkaḷum iv veṇṇūliṉ varappukkuṭpaṭṭut tōṟṟuvaṉavē eṉpatu tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ tuṇipākum. 1
iṉik kalviyiṉ iraṇṭāvatu pirivākac collappaṭṭa eḻutteṉpatu, iyaṟṟamiḻ nūlākum. ulakil uḷḷa poruḷ ellāvaṟṟiyum uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi eṉa iraṇṭākap pakuttu, avaṟṟiṉ iyalpiṉi uḷḷavāṟu viḷakkuvatu, iyaṟṟamiḻiṉ iyalpākum. ulakattār ceyyum viṉikaḷ yāvum aṟivatum ceyvatum eṉa iraṇṭuḷ aṭaṅkum. avaṟṟuḷ eṇṇi aṟivataṟkuk karuviyāvaṉa eṇ (aṟiviyal) nūl eṉavum, aṟintu ceyvataṟkut tuṇi ceyvaṉa eḻuttu (iyal) nūl eṉavum paṇṭit tamiḻ makkaḷāl vaḻaṅkap peṟṟaṉa. eṇṇūṟpayiṟciyāl epporuḷiyum teḷiya viḷakkum urit tiṟamum, iyal nūṟpayiṟciyāl ettoḻili yum ceytu muṭikkum ceyaṟṟiṟamum perukumeṉpatu paṇṭit tamiḻāciriyarkaḷiṉ karuttākum.
eṇṇūlkaḷiṉ iyalpuṇarntu ōtum muṟiyiṉi `vāyiṉāl vakutta pakkam eṉavum, iyal nūlkaḷiṉ karut tuṇarntu ariya viṉikaḷik kiyāl iyaṟṟum muṟiyiṉik `kiyiṉāl vakutta pakkam eṉavum tamiḻc cāṉṟōr vakip paṭuttiyuḷḷaṉar. eṇṇūliṉi aṟiviyal (Science) nūl eṉavum, iyal nūliṉik kali (Arts) nūl eṉavum ikkāli mēli nāṭṭāciriyarkaḷ vakutturippataṉi otta muṟi nam tamiḻ moḻiyil toṉṟu toṭṭuk kāṇappaṭṭu varuvatu, tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ uyarnta kalvit tiṟaṉip pulappaṭuttuvatākum.
eṇṇum eḻuttumākiya iruvakik kalviyiṉiyum tamiḻ makkaḷ taṅkaḷukkuriya iraṇṭu kaṇkaḷākak karutip pōṟṟi vantārkaḷ. makkaḷ uṭampilē aminta kaṇkaḷ oru kālattē ōriṭattē aminta puṟapporuḷkaḷiyē kāṭṭuvaṉa. eṇṇum eḻuttumākiya ikkaṇkaḷō, iṭattiyum kālattiyum ūṭuruvic ceṉṟu, ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum kāṇa valla ciṟappuṭiyaṉavākum. ātalāl, makkaḷ uyirkku ivviraṇṭumē ciṟanta kaṇkaḷām eṉat tamiḻc cāṉṟōr karutiṉar. aṟivillā tār ivviru tiṟak kalviyiyum eṇ eṉṟum eḻuttoṉṟum kūṟit tamakkup puṟampākak karuti vantālum, aṟintavar ivviraṇṭaṉiyum ciṟappuṭiya uyirkaḷukkuk kaṇkaḷ eṉṟē aṟivuṟutti vantaṉar.
eṇṇeṉpa ēṉi eḻutteṉpa ivviraṇṭum
 kaṇṇeṉpa vāḻu muyirkku.
eṉṟa tirukkuṟaḷālum (392) ataṟkup parimēlaḻakar eḻutiya uriyālum ivvuṇmi pulaṉām.
kallātār mukattiṟ kaṇṇuṭiyarāyiṉum, ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷiṉ iyalpiṉi uḷḷavāṟuṇarum ñāṉak kaṇṇip peṟāmiyāl, avartam mukattiluḷḷa kaṇkaḷ puṇkaḷākavē iḻitturikkappaṭṭaṉa.
kaṟkappaṭum nūlkaḷip paḻutaṟak kaṟṟu, kaṟṟapaṭi naṉṉeṟikkaṇ oḻukutal vēṇṭumeṉavum, tōṇṭat tōṇṭak kiṇaṟṟil nīr ūṟuvatu pōla, nūlkaḷi mēlum mēlum kaṟṟalāl aṟivu perukum eṉavum, aḻivillāta celvam kalviyē eṉavum, tām kaṟṟa kalvi tamakkēyaṉṟi, ulakattārkkum iṉpantaruvataṉik kaṇṭu, kaṟṟōr mēlum akkalviyiṉiyē virumpuvar eṉavum, kaṟṟavaṉukkut taṉṉāṭum taṉṉūrumēyaṉṟi. ennāṭum evvūrum  taṉṉāṭum taṉṉūrumē yākum eṉavum, orupiṟappilē kaṟṟakalvi oruvarkku eḻupiṟappi lum uyirōṭu ceṉṟu utavum iyalpuṭiyatātalāl, ak kalviyi oruvaṉ iṟakkumaḷavum kaṟṟal vēṇṭumeṉavum āciriyar tiruvaḷḷuvaṉār aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟār.
tirukkuṟaḷiluḷḷa kalvi, kallāmi, kēḷvi, aṟivuṭimi, col vaṉmi eṉṉum aintatikāraṅkaḷum kalvip payiṟciyiṉ muṟiyiṉiyē iṉitu viḷakkuvaṉavām. kalviyiṉ muṭinta elliyāka āciriyarāl aṟivuṟuttap peṟuvatu, `meyyuṇartal eṉṉum atikāramākum. tuṟavaṟaviyaliṟ kūṟappaṭṭa ivvatikāram, tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ camaya nūṟpayiṟciyiṉi naṉku teḷivippatākum.
tamiḻ makkaḷ ivvulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷi mutaṟporuḷ, karupporuḷ, uripporuḷ eṉa mūvakiyākap pakuttu ārāyntārkaḷ. avaṟṟuḷ mutaṟporuḷ eṉappaṭuvaṉa, iṭamum kālamumām. iviyiraṇṭaṉ cārpākat tōṉṟum pul maram mutaliya niliyiyaṟ poruḷkaḷum, paṟavi vilaṅku mutaliya iyaṅkiyaṟ poruḷkaḷum, uṇavu toḻil mutaliyaṉa vum karup poruḷkaḷām. ivi yāvum nilamum kālamumākiya mutaṟporuḷiṉkaṇṇē karukkoṇṭu tōṟṟuvaṉavātalāl, karupporuḷkaḷeṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟṟaṉa. makkaḷukkuriya akamum puṟamumākiya oḻukalāṟukaḷ, uripporuḷ eṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟṟaṉa.
ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷiyellām mutal, karu, uri eṉa mūṉṟu tiṟamākap pakuttukkoṇṭu tamiḻ makkaḷ kaṇṭaṟinta nuṭpaṅkaḷ yāvum avarkaḷatu kalvip payiṟciyi mikutip paṭuttiṉa. nilam, nīr, tī, kāṟṟu, ākāyam eṉṉum aimperum pūtaṅkaḷāl ākiyatē ivvulakam eṉṉum uṇmiyiṉip pallāyira āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē nam tamiḻ muṉṉōr uṇarntu veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷaṉar.
ivvulakaṉittiṉiyum, itaṉic cūḻnta veḷiyākiya vāṉattiṉ iyalpiṉiyum, vāṉattil iyaṅkum oḷip poruḷkaḷākiya ñāyiṟu tiṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiyum ārāyn tuṇarum ārāycciyil tamiḻ makkaḷ mikap paḻiya kālattilēyē īṭupaṭṭu uḻittuḷḷārkaḷ. avvuḻippiṉ payaṉāka avarkaḷ kaṇṭuṇarntu veḷiyiṭṭa uṇmikaḷ palavākum. avarkaḷ pūtanūl ārāycci paṟṟi iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē aṟivuṟuttiya uṇmikaḷiṟ cila ippoḻutu kiṭittuḷḷa caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷil āṅkāṅkē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. toṉmik kālattilēyē tamiḻ makkaḷ kaṇṭuṇarttiya muṭivukaḷ ik kālattup palvēṟu karuvikaḷiṉ tuṇi koṇṭu mēṉāṭṭār kaṇṭuṇarttiya muṭivukaḷuṭaṉ peritum ottirukkak kāṇkiṉṟōm.
ñāyiṟākiya aṉaṟpiḻampiliruntu teṟittu viḻunta oru ciṟu uruṇṭiyē ulakameṉṟum, innilattiṉiṉṟum teṟittuc ceṉṟa maṟṟōr uruṇṭiyē cantiraṉeṉṟum, ñāyiṟṟiṉiṉṟum teṟittu viḻunta pūmi ataṉatu kavarcciyāl iḻukkappaṭṭu ñāyiṟṟic cuṟṟivarukiṟateṉṟum, avvāṟē pūmiyiliruntu citaṟiya maṟṟōr uruṇṭiyākiya cantiraṉ ñāyiṟṟiyum taṉakkup piṟappiṭamākiya pūmiyiyum oruṅku cuṟṟi varukiṟa teṉṟum ikkāla ārācciyāḷar kūṟuvar. vicumpil mēṉ mēṟceṉṟu pārkkuṅkāl, aṅkuk kāṟṟiṉ iyakkam kuṟintuḷḷa teṉpatum, oru kuṟippiṭṭa ellikku mēl kāṟṟu aṟavē iyaṅkuvatilli eṉpatum ikkālattār kaṇṭa ārāycci yiṉāl uṟuti ceyyap peṟṟa uṇmikaḷākum.
ivvuṇmiyiṉi iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē taṅkaḷ nuṇṇuṇarvāl aṟintu veḷippaṭuttiya pēraṟiñarkaḷ tamiḻ nāṭṭil vāḻntārkaḷ. appēraṟiñar kaḷiṉ kalvittiṟatti nēriṟkaṇṭu viyanta uṟiyūr mutu kaṇṇaṉ cāttaṉār eṉṉum pulavar perumāṉ, avarkaḷatu kalvit tiṟattiṉ uyarviṉip paṟṟi eṭutturippatāka aminta pāṭaloṉṟu puṟanāṉūṟṟil uḷḷatu.
ñāyiṟṟiṉatu iyakkamum, ataṉuṭiya īrppāṟṟalum, avvāṟṟalāl iḻukkappaṭṭu añ ñāyiṟṟic cuṟṟi varum ippār vaṭṭamum, ippūmiyil kāṟṟu vīcum ticiyum, kāṟṟiṉṟi vaṟitēyuḷḷa ākāyamum eṉṟu collappaṭṭa ivaṟṟi aṅkaṅkē ceṉṟu nēriṟkaṇṭaṟintavarkaḷip pōlac `ceṉṟa kālattu ivvāṟu iruntaṉa; ippoḻutu iṉṉa niliyil uḷḷaṉa; iṉi etir kālattu iṉṉa taṉmiyi aṭivaṉa; eṉṟu mūṉṟu kālattum aṟutiyiṭṭuc collum kalviyi yuṭiya periyārkaḷum innāṭṭil uḷḷārkaḷ, eṉpār,
ceññā yiṟṟuc celavum añ ñāyiṟṟup
 parippum parippuc cūḻnta maṇṭilamum
 vaḷitiritaru ticiyum
 vaṟitu niliiya kāyamu meṉṟivi
 ceṉṟaḷan taṟintōr pōla veṉṟum
 iṉitteṉ pōrum uḷarē.	(puṟam. 30)
eṉak kūṟukiṉṟār.
ñāyiṟṟiṉ parippiṉāl (iḻuppāṟṟalāl) pūmi ataṉic cūḻntu varutalum, vāṉam kāṟṟiṉṟi iruttalum ākiya uṇmikaḷ ippāṭalil kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉa. ikkāla ārāycci yāḷar nuṇṇiya karuvikaḷik koṇṭu kaṇṭaṟinta iv vuṇmikaḷi uṟiyūr mutukaṇṇaṉ cāttaṉār iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē kūṟiyuḷḷami caṅkakālat tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ vāṉa nūṟpayiṟciyiṉi iṉitu viḷakkuvatākum.
vāṉattil miṉṉukiṉṟa oḷipporuḷkaḷi uṟṟu nōkki avaṟṟiṟku `mīṉkaḷ eṉap peyariṭṭaṉar. immīṉkaḷi `nāḷmīṉ, kōḷmīṉ eṉa iruvakiyākap pakutturippār. tampāluḷḷa iyaṟkiyoḷiyāl viṭṭu viṭṭu miṉṉuvaṉa nāḷ mīṉkaḷ eṉṟum, piṟavaṟṟiṉ oḷiyik koṇṭu viḷaṅkuvaṉa kōḷ mīṉkaḷ eṉṟum tamiḻ muṉṉōr peyariṭṭu vaḻaṅkiṉami avarkaḷatu vāṉanūṟ payiṟciyi naṉku viḷakkum.
kūṭalūr kiḻār eṉṉum pulavar, yāṉikkaṭcēy māntarañcēral irumpoṟi eṉṟa cēramaṉṉaṉ kālattil vāḻntavar. ivar kālattil paṅkuṉit tiṅkaḷiṉ mutaṟ patiṉintil kārttiki nāḷil uttaram ucciyiliruntucāya, mūlameḻa, mirukacīriṭam maṟiya, naṭcattiram oṉṟu vaṭakkum kiḻakkum pōkāmal iṭinaṭuvē erintu vīḻntateṉṟum; ataṉik kaṇṭa kūṭalur kiḻār eṉṉum ippulavar tam aracaṉākiya yāṉik kaṭcēy māntarañ cēralirum poṟikkut tīṅkuṇṭākumeṉṟu terintu uḷantiṭukkuṟṟār eṉṟum; pulavar añciyataṟkēṟpavē aṉṟikku ēḻāvatu nāḷil ammaṉṉaṉ uyirnīttāṉ eṉṟum avar pāṭiya puṟanāṉūṟṟup pāṭaloṉṟāl (229) aṟikiṉṟōm.
kūṭalūr kiḻār pāṭiya puṟappāṭṭiluḷḷa vāṉa nūṟ kuṟiyīṭṭuc coṟkaḷ yāvum taṉit tamiḻt coṟkaḷākavē amintuḷḷataṉi nōkkuṅkāl, avarkālattu vāṉa nūlip paṟṟiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷ pala iyaṟṟappaṭṭiruntami naṉku pulaṉām. vāṉa nūliṟ ciṟanta payiṟciyuṭiyār `kaṇi, eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭuvar. pakalum iravum iṭiviṭāmal ākāyattip pārttu, aṅkē nikaḻum vāṉavil, miṉṉal, ūrkōḷ (parivēṭam), tūmam, mīṉ vīḻval, kōḷ nili, maḻi nili mutaliyavaṟṟik kaṇṇi (karuti) p payaṉ kūṟuvār kaṇi eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭuvar. kaṇiyaṉ pūṅkuṉṟaṉār eṉṉum pulavar cōtiṭattil vallavar eṉpatu avar `kaṇiyaṉ eṉa vaḻaṅkap paṭutalāl viḷaṅkum. caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷil vāṉa nūlippaṟṟiya pala kuṟippukkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ak kuṟippukkaḷit toṭarntu ārāyntāl, vāṉa nūlpaṟṟiya putiya uṇmikaḷ pala veḷippaṭutal kūṭum.
tamiḻ makkaḷ nilattiṉ taṭpa veppa iyalpiṉi naṉkuṇarntu, annilattiṉik kuṟiñci, mulli, marutam, neytal eṉa ivvāṟu vakuttu, avaṟṟiṟkēṟṟa kār, kūtir, muṉpaṉi, piṉpaṉi, iḷavēṉil, mutuvēṉil eṉa aṟuvakip perumpoḻutukaḷum; māli, yāmam, vikaṟi, kāli, naṇpakal, eṟpāṭu eṉa aṟuvakic ciṟu poḻutukaḷum vakutturittalāl, nila nūl paṟṟiya kalvi akkālat tamiḻ makkaḷukku aṟivuṟuttap peṟṟami pulaṉām. nilaṅkaḷuk kēṟṟavāṟu pul maram mutaliya tāvaraṅkaḷum, paṟavi vilaṅku mutaliya uyir vakikaḷum iṉṉiṉṉa eṉap pirittu viḷakkutalāl, tāvara nūlum uyir nūlum paṟṟiya uṇmikaḷ akkālattuk kaṟpikkappaṭṭami teḷiyappaṭum.
nilattiṟkēṟṟa uṇavum toḻilum vakutturittalāl, nilaviyalpukkēṟpa mēṟkoḷḷum payirnūl payiṟṟappaṭṭatu eṉa uyattuṇaralām. kuṟiñci mutaliya nilaṅkaḷukkut takkapaṭi avvannilaṅkaḷilē vāḻum makkaḷuṭiya uḷḷattilē ciṟantu tōṉṟum uḷḷattuṇarccikaḷ iṉṉa eṉat tamiḻiyal nūlākiya tolkāppiyam kūṟukiṉṟatu. ivvāṟu nilattiṟkum aṅku vāḻum makkaḷiṉ uḷḷattuṇarccikkumuḷḷa toṭarpiṉik kaṇṭuṇarntamiyāl, makkaḷatu uḷḷattiṉ iyalpiṉi uṇarvatil tamiḻar niṟinta tērcciyuṭiyār eṉpatu naṉku tuṇiyappaṭum.
ulakattiṉ tōṟṟam, uyirttōṟṟam, ulaka oṭukkam, aracar vaḻimuṟi, kālavariyaṟi, maṉṉar varalāṟṟuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭiya nikaḻccikaḷ eṉṉum ipporuḷkaḷi virit turippatu ulaka varalāṟākum. ivvaralāṟṟiṉi uṇarntavarē muṉṉuḷḷa pēraṟiñarkaḷ aṟivuṟuttiya kalic celvatti naṉkurum ūkkamum āṟṟalum peṟṟavarāvar. ittakiya ulaka varalāṟu paṇṭi nāḷil tamiḻk kalviyil iṭam peṟṟiruntatu. ivvaralāṟu urinaṭiyil amintāltāṉ ellā makkaḷukkum payaṉpaṭum eṉpatu muṉṉuḷḷōr karuttākum. ivvāṟu urinaṭiyil aminta paḻiya varalāṟu `toṉmi eṉṟa collāl vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. 
aṟattiṉ muṟi tavaṟātu tām ceyyum toḻilāl poruḷīṭṭi iṉpam nukartaṟkuriya vāḻkkiyiṉ iyalpiṉi akam, puṟam eṉa iraṇṭu pirivāka vakuttu viḷakkuvatē tamiḻukkuc ciṟappāka uḷḷa poruḷilakkaṇa nūlākum. makkaḷ vāḻkkiyiṉ iyalpiṉi uḷḷavāṟu viḷakkiyurikkum vāḻviyal nūlākiya poruḷilakkaṇap pakuti, tamiḻ moḻiyi laṉṟi vēṟu emmoḻiyilum kāṇappaṭāta ciṟappuṭiyatākum.
makkaḷuṭiya vāḻkkikku araṇ ceyvatu araciyal nūṟkalviyākum. araciyaliṉ iyalpiṉi āciriyar tiruvaḷḷuvaṉār tirukkuṟaḷ poruṭpālil teḷivāka aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟār. tirukkuṟaḷi naṉku kaṟṟuṇarvōr caṅkakālat tamiḻakattil vaḻaṅkiya araciyal muṟiyiṉ cemmi yiṉi naṉkuṇarvar. nāṭṭiṉi āḷum araciyaṟṟalivaṉ taṉṉāṭṭukku uṇṭākum ākkattiyum, ataṉip peṟutaṟkut taṭiyāyuḷḷa iṭiyūṟu kaḷiyum puṟattārkkup pulappaṭātu taṉ maṉattēyaṭakkik koṇṭu amitiyāka araciyalic celuttutaṟkuriyavaṉāvaṉ. cōḻaṉ nalaṅkiḷḷi eṉṟa maṉṉaṉ, yāṉi taṉ mēl oruvaṉ eṟinta kalli yārukkum pulappaṭāmal taṉ kaṉṉattil aṭakkik koṇṭiruntāṟpōlak, kālamum iṭaṉum karuti aṭaṅkiyiruntāṉ. avaṉatu araciyal nuṭpattiṉi uṟiyūr mutukaṇṇaṉ cāttaṉār eṉṉum pulavar oru ceyyuḷiṟ pukaḻntu pōṟṟukiṉṟār:
aimperumpūtattiṉ iyalpiṉiyum, ñāyiṟu mutaliya vaṟṟiṉ iyakkattiyum nērē ceṉṟu pārttavarkaḷip pōla nāḷum iṉṉa taṉmiyaṉa eṉac colkiṉṟa pēraṟiñarkaḷum inta nāṭṭil vāḻkiṉṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷālum aṟiya muṭiyāta vaṉmiyiṉiyuṭiyavaṉāka nī aṭaṅki vāḻkiṉṟāy. ātalāl, niṉṉuṭiya tiṟatti evvāṟu pulavarkaḷ viḷaṅkappāṭuvārkaḷ? eṉa avaṉi avar pōṟṟum muṟi, avvēntaṉatu araciyaṟ kalviyiṉ nuṭpattik kuṟippāka aṟivuṟuttuvatātal kāṇalām.
vāḻkkikku iṉṟiyamiyātu vēṇṭappaṭuvatu poruḷākum. `poruḷ illārkku ivvulaka vāḻvil yātoru toṭarpum illi eṉat tiruvaḷḷuvar kūṟukiṉṟār. poruḷkaḷ varutaṟkuriya vaḻi tuṟikaḷi mēlum mēlum uṇṭākku talilum, attuṟikaḷiṟ kiṭitta poruḷkaḷi oru vaḻit tokuttalilum, am mutaṟporuḷ aḻiyātu mēṉmēl vaḷarakkūṭiya niliyiṟ pātukāttalilum, avvāṟu kākkappeṟṟa poruḷkaḷi nāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ nalaṅkaruti aṟam poruḷ iṉpaṅkaḷiṉporuṭṭuc celavaḻittalilum vallavaṉē aracaṉāvaṉ, eṉpatu tiruvaḷḷuvar aṟivuṟuttiya poruḷuri yākum. ipporuḷuriyiṉic ciṟitu ūṉṟi nōkku vōmāṉāl, caṅkakālattil poruḷātāra vāḻviṉic cemmippaṭuttum nūlkaḷ pala vaḻaṅkiṉami naṉku pulaṉām. poruḷātāra nūl akkālattup `poruḷ purinūl eṉṟa peyarāl vaḻaṅkap peṟṟatu. nāṭṭil uḷḷa poruḷ, cakaṭakkāl pōla yāvarkaṇṇum ceṉṟu payaṉ tarum āṟṟaluṭiya tākum. apporuḷiṉ vaḷarcciyiṉi uṇarnta aṟiñarkaḷ ataṉi mutalākak koṇṭu ētāvatoru toḻilic ceytu ūtiyam perukkuvatil karuttic celuttiṉārkaḷ. `mutaṟ poruḷip peṟṟārkkē ūtiyam uṇṭu; mutalillārkku ūtiyam illi eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar. tampāl uḷḷa poruḷip poṉṉiṉum maṇiyiṉum ākiya aṇikalaṅ kaḷukkuc celaviṭuvōr, apporuḷiṉ āṟṟaliyuṇarātu ataṉi muṭakki nōy ceypavarāvar. innuṭpattiṉic caṅkakālattup potumakkaḷ naṉṟāka uṇarntiruntārkaḷ. `āyarkulap peṇṇorutti tāṉ mōr viṟṟataṉāl vāṅkiya nel mutaliya uṇavup poruḷ kaḷik koṇṭu taṉ cuṟṟattārellāriyum pātukāttup piṉpu tāṉ neyyi viṟkiṉṟa vilip poruḷukkuk kaṭṭiyākap poṉṉi vāṅkik koḷḷāmal, tāṉ ceyyum pāl mōr vāṇikatti mēlum perukkutaṟkuriya pālerumiyiyum, nalla pacuviyum, kariya erumi nākiṉiyum vāṅkiṉāḷ, eṉṟa ceyti perumpāṇāṟṟup paṭiyil kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. itaṉi uyttuṇarumiṭattu makaḷirum ēṟṟup pōṟṟutaṟkuriya niliyil poruḷpurinūṟ pulami tamiḻar ellāriṭattum amin tiruntami pulaṉām.
ivvāṟu pala tuṟiyilum kalvi vaḷarcciyaṭiyavē, kaṟṟa nūṟporuḷ paṟṟik kaṟṟāriṭiyē karuttu vēṟupāṭukaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. avaravarum tām tām karutiyaṉavē uṇmiyeṉa nili nāṭṭa muyaṉṟaṉar. inniliyil palarum uṭaṉpaṭṭu ēṟṟuk koḷḷutaṟkuriya neṟiyil poruḷiṉ tuṇipuṇarttum aḷavi nūlkaḷ vakukkap peṟṟaṉa. avvaḷavi nūṉmuṟiyip piṉpaṟṟic cāṉṟōr palarum tammuṭ poruḷvēṟṟumi karuti uṟaḻntu pēcit tām karutiya poruḷi viḷakka muyaṉṟaṉar. ivvāṟu kalvit tuṟiyil tannikaraṟṟa pulami peṟṟa cāṉṟōr, tam karuttiṉi nili nāṭṭak karuti, ‘emmi uṟaḻntu kūṟuvāruḷarāyiṉ varuka! eṉap pēraviyiṟ koṭi kaṭṭi aḻittali vaḻakkamākak koṇṭaṉar. kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattil kalvittuṟiyil paṟṟi uṟaḻcci kuṟittuc cāṉṟōr koṭi kaṭṭiyiruntaṉar.

`palkēḷvit tuṟipōkiya
tollāṇi nallāciriyar
uṟaḻkuṟit teṭutta uyarkeḻu koṭi
eṉa varum paṭṭiṉappāli aṭikaḷāl attakiya koṭiyiṉ iyalpiṉi naṉkuṇaralām. `palavākiya nūṟkēḷviyiṉiyum, muṟṟak kaṟṟuc colliyapaṭi naṭakkum paḻiya āṇimoḻi yiṉiyumuṭiya nallāciriyarkaḷ vātu ceyyak karutik kaṭṭiṉa accam poruntiṉa koṭi eṉpatu mēṟkāṭṭiya aṭikaḷiṉ poruḷākum.
iraṭṭitta katirkaḷiyuṭiyatāki viḷinta tiṇiyiṉ tōṟṟatti viḷakka vanta pulavaroruvar, tarukka neṟiyiṉip piṉpaṟṟip poruḷi viḷakkik kāṭṭupavaṉ kiyiṭattu iru pirivāka iṇinta viralkaḷi uvamiyākak kūṟi viḷakku kiṉṟār. (malipaṭu.) ivvāṟu nūṟ poruḷkaḷi viḷakkutaṟkuc colvaṉmi iṉṟiyamiyātatākum. oruvaṉ taṉ uḷḷak karuttukkaḷit taṅku taṭiyiṉṟic collāl veḷip paṭuttum moḻip payiṟciyē colvaṉmi eṉappaṭum. `tām kaṟṟa nūṟ poruḷip piṟar aṟiyumpaṭi viritturikkum colvaṉmi illātavar, naṉṟāka malarntum maṇaṅkamaḻāta malarip pōṉṟu payaṉpaṭātavarāvar, eṉa aṟiñr kūṟuvar. oruvaṉ tāṉ kūṟak karutiya poruḷkaḷ piṟarāl uṇarntu koḷḷutaṟku ariyaṉa vāyiṉum, kēṭpārkku eḷiya poruḷāka maṉattiṟ patiyumpaṭi teḷivāṉa coṟkaḷāl viḷakki yurittal vēṇṭum. piṟar kūṟukiṉṟa poruḷkaḷ teḷivāka viḷaṅkik koḷḷa iyalāta ariya poruḷkaḷāyiṉum, avar kūṟum coṟkaḷiṉ nuṇṇiya poruḷkaḷi uyttuṇarntu kāṇutal vēṇṭum. tāṉ kaṟṟavaṟṟip piṟarkku viḷaṅka uṇarttum muṟiyum, piṟar kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟittāṉ viḷaṅka uṇarum muṟiyumākiya ivviru tiṟaṉumuṭiyatē aṟivu eṉa āciriyar tiruvaḷḷuvar ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟukiṉṟār. 
`oruvaruṭiya aṟiviṉ māṭciyiṉi viḷaṅka uṇarttum karuviyāvaṉa, avartam vāyc coṟkaḷē, eṉa akkālat tamiḻar naṉku tuṇintaṉar. avarkaḷ colvaṉmiyiṉi vaḷarttaṟkup payaṉpaṭṭatu `urinūl eṉpatākum. vaḻakkiyal nūl caṅkakālat tamiḻ makaḷirkkup payiṟṟappaṭṭatu. kāvirip pūmpaṭṭiṉattilaminta aṟaṅ kūṟaviyattilē ivvuri nūl vaḻaṅkap peṟṟateṉavum ivvuri nūl neṟikku māṟupaṭap pēcutal ākāteṉavum iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ kūṟukiṉṟār.
ōviyam eḻutum muṟiyiṉik kaṟpikkum ōviya nūlum, maṉi mutaliyaṉa amikkum ciṟpa nūlum, makkaḷuṭiya uṭampiluḷḷa nōyiṉit tīrkkum maruttuva nūlum, ivvāṟē vēṟu pala nuṇkalikaḷi aṟivikkum nūlkaḷum, caṅka kālattil vaḻaṅkiṉa. āciriyar tiruvaḷḷuvaṉār `maruntu eṉṟa atikārattil aṟivuṟuttiya uṇmikaḷi ārāyumiṭattu avar kālattil  uṭampiṉ amitiyiṉi uḷḷavāṟu viḷakkiya uṭal nūlum, uṭaliluḷḷa nōykaḷit tīrttaṟkuriya maruttuva muṟiyiṉi viḷakkum maruttuva nūlum vaḻaṅkiyiruttal vēṇṭumeṉat teḷital eḷitām. `nalloḻukkattāl tiruttamākap pōṟṟi varappaṭṭa uṭampil maruttuvaṉ koṭutta maruntu viriviṟceṉṟu cērntu nalla payaṉit tarum, eṉap peruṅkaṭuṅkō eṉṉum pulavar kūṟukiṉṟār. (pālikkali. 16) itaṉāl, uṭampiṉ kūṟupāṭuṇarntu maruntu ceyyum muṟi caṅkakālattil vaḻaṅkiyatu pulappaṭum.
itukāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟāl eṇṇum eḻuttumākiya iru vakiyilaṭaṅkum palvēṟu kalvit tuṟikaḷip paṟṟiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷum caṅkakālattu vaḻaṅkiṉami oruvāṟu teḷintu koḷḷalām.
ici
tamiḻ moḻikkē uriyaṉavākat tamiḻ makkaḷ vakut tuṇarttiyavi iciyum nāṭakamum ākum. iyaṟkiyil eḻunta iṉiya ociyip poruḷuṇarttum pākkaḷōṭu oṉṟupaṭutti icittup pāṭuvatu `ici eṉappaṭum. mūlātārattiṟṟoṭaṅkiya eḻuttiṉ oli, neñcu, kaṇṭam, nākku, mūkku, mēlvāy, kīḻvāy, utaṭu, pal eṉṉum eṭṭiṭaṅ kaḷilum porunti, eṭuttal, paṭuttal, nalital, uṟaḻvu, kampitam, oli, uruṭṭu, tākku eṉṉum eṇ vakippaṭṭa toḻilkaḷāl uruvākap paṇṇik koḷḷappaṭutalāṟ `paṇ eṉpatum kāraṇap peyarām. nāṉilattavarākiya nilamāntar ellārum, tattam nilattiṟkuc ciṟappurimiyuṭiya yāḻ, paṇ, paṟi mutaliya icik kūṟupāṭukaḷi vaḷarttu vantaṉar. kiḷi, kural mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ iṉkuraliṉik kēṭṭuṇarnta makkaḷ, iyaṟkiyil tōṉṟum avviṉṉōcikaḷi yotta ōcikaḷit tammuḷḷiruntu eḻuppak kaṟṟuk koṇṭatuṭaṉ paṭippaṭiyāka mēṉmēl uyarntu cellum avvōciyiṉi ēḻu vakiyākap pakuttuk koṇṭārkaḷ. āṇ makkaḷatu kaṇṭattiṉ ōci kāntāra curattiyotta teṉpar. accuruttiṉ vaḻiyōciyāka `uḻi eṉṟa ociyum, ataṉ vaḻiyōciyākak `kural eṉṟa ōciyum ataṉ vaḻiyōciyāka `iḷi eṉṟa ōciyum, ataṉiyoṭṭit `tuttam eṉṟa ōciyum, ataṉiyoṭṭi `viḷari eṉṟa ōciyum, ataṉ vaḻiyākak `kikkiḷi eṉṟa ōciyum kiḷimuṟiyākap piṟakkum eṉa mutaṉmutaṟ kaṇṭuṇarnta perumi tamiḻ makkaḷukkē uriyatākum.
ivvēḻicikaḷiyum aṭippaṭiyāka vittuk koṇṭu tamiḻ makkaḷāl vaḷarkkap peṟṟatē icit tamiḻākum. icik kaliyip piṟaḻāta niliyil vaḷarppaṉa, kuḻal yāḻ mutaliya icik karuvikaḷē. mūṅkilil vaṇṭukaḷ tuḷitta tuḷikaḷiṉ vaḻiyē kāṟṟup pukuntu iyaṅka, attuḷikaḷiliruntu uṇṭākiya iṉṉōciyiṉi mikap paḻiya kālattilē kaṇṭu kēṭṭuṇarnta tamiḻ muṉṉōr, tam uṇarviṉ tiṟattāl mutaṉ mutal amittukkoṇṭa icik karuvi kuḻalākum. villiṉ kaṇṇē valintu kaṭṭappaṭṭa nāṇōciyiṉ iṉimiyi uṇarntu kumiḻaṅkompil maral nārāl toṭuttuk koṇṭa paḻiya icik karuvi vil yāḻ eṉpatākum. variyaṟukka muṭiyāta toṉmik kālattilē aminta ivvil yāḻiṉi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭē `cemmuṟik kēḷvi yeṉṉum `cakōṭa yāḻ mutalāka āyira narampuṭiya `peruṅkalam eṉṉum `pēriyāḻ īṟākavuḷḷa ciṟanta icik karuvikaḷellām uyttuṇarntu amittuk koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. ovvoru curattiṟkum taṉittaṉi narampu kaṭṭap peṟṟatu tamiḻar kaṇṭa yāḻk karuviyākum. oru narampilē pala curaṅkaḷiyum vācittaṟ kamintatu piṟkālattu vīṇik karuviyākum. ivvīṇik karuvi orē muṟiyil toṭarntu cellum toṭariciyiṉi maṭṭumē vācittaṟ kuriyatu. tamiḻaratu yāḻk karuviyō, toṭariciyuṭaṉ palarum cērntu pāṭum paṇṇākiya otticiyiṉiyum vācittaṟku ēṟpuṭiyatākum. toṭariciyiṉi maṭṭum vācittaṟkuriya vīṇik karuviyik kāṭṭilum, toṭariciyiṉiyum otticiyiṉiyum oru cēra vācittaṟkaminta yāḻkkaruvi peritum ciṟappuṭiyateṉpatu collāmalē viḷaṅkum. akkālattu yāḻukku narampu kaṭṭutal vaḻakkam. narampu veyil maḻi mutaliyavaṟṟāl ēṟpaṭum nekiḻcci iṟukkam kāraṇamāka ici vēṟupaṭum nīrmiyatu. paṇṭit tamiḻar kuḻalōciyiṉ tuṇikoṇṭē yāḻ narampukku ici kūṭṭiṉar. kuḻal, yāḻ eṉpaṉa taṉittamiḻc coṟkaḷām.
tamiḻ makkaḷ kuḻalum yāḻumākiya icik karuvikaḷiṉ tuṇikoṇṭu palvēṟu ici nuṭpaṅkaḷi veḷiyiṭ ṭuḷḷārkaḷ eṉpatu muttamiḻk kāppiyamākiya cilappatikārat tiṉālum, ataṉ urikaḷālum naṉku tuṇiyappaṭum. iṭik kālattil vaḻakkaṟṟu maṟinta yāḻkkaruviyiṉ iyalpum, ataṉkaṇ icikkap peṟṟa paṉṉiru pālikaḷiṉ payaṉum, kuṟiñci pāli mulli marutam eṉṉum nāṟperumpaṇkaḷiṉ iyalpum, avaṟṟiṉ vaḻi aminta nūṟṟu mūṉṟu paṇkaḷiṉ tiṟaṉum ākiya ici nuṭpaṅkaḷi ikkālattilum payaṉpaṭutti makiḻum vaṇṇam cēra muṉivarākiya iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ aṟivuṟuttiya tiṟattiṉi aruṇmiku vipulānanta aṭikaḷār  tām iyaṟṟiya icit tamiḻ nūlākiya yāḻnūlil teḷivāka aṟivuṟuttiyuḷḷārkaḷ. ulaka icikaḷ ellāvaṟṟiṟkum paḻantamiḻiciyē aṭippaṭiyāy aminta uṇmiyiṉi yāḻnūliṟkaṇṭu teḷital tamiḻaṟiñar kaṭaṉākum.
oṉṟōṭoṉṟu toṭarpillāta taṉittaṉi varalāṟṟu nikaḻccikaḷi naṭittuk kāṭṭutal `kūttu eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭum. iṟivaṉ, murukaṉ, māyōṉ, koṟṟavi mutaliya teyvaṅkaḷiṉ vīrac ceyalkaḷi viḷakkik kāṭṭum patiṉōrāṭalkaḷ kūttu eṉṉum ivvakiyic cērntaṉavām. patiṉōrāṭaliṉ iyalpu cilappatikārattil viḷakkappaṭṭatu. ittakiya kūttiṉ iyalpiṉi viḷakkiya nūl `kūttanūl eṉa vaḻaṅkappeṟum.
makkaḷ oruvarip pōṉṟu oruvar ottu naṭittal `porunutal eṉṟa collāṟ kuṟikkappaṭum. ittoḻilil valla āṭavar `porunar eṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟṟaṉar. makaḷir tam uḷḷakkuṟippu uṭampil tōṉṟum meyppāṭukaḷāl pulappaṭum paṭi cuvipaṭ naṭittal `nāṭṭiyam eṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟṟatu. itaṟku vēṇṭum āṭal, pāṭal, aḻaku eṉpavaṟṟi viḷakki urikkum nūl `nāṭṭiya naṉṉūl eṉa iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷāṟ kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. mātavi cōḻamaṉṉaṉ pēraviyil āṭiya āṭaṟkalikaḷ yāvum nāṭṭiya naṉṉūli naṉku kaṭippiṭittuk kāṭṭiyaṉavākumeṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ kūṟukiṉṟār. uḷḷatum illatumākiya ētāvatoru kati yiṉit taḻuvi naṭikkap peṟuvatu `nāṭakam eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭum. uriyum pāṭṭumāy amintu naṭittaṟkup payaṉpaṭum muṟi yiluḷḷa kāppiyam `nāṭakak kāppiyam eṉak kuṟikkappaṭṭatu.
āṭalic collikkoṭukkum āciriyaṉ iyalpum, iciyāciriyaṉ iyalpum, nāṭakattiṟkum icikkum vēṇṭiya icippāṭalkaḷi iyaṟṟit tarum kaviñaṉ iyalpum, iciyiṉi varampu peṟa niṟutta valla taṇṇumi (mattaḷa) āciriyaṉ toḻiṟṟiṟaṉum, kuḻalicippāṉ amitiyum, yāḻāciriyaṉ iyalpum, nāṭaka araṅkiṉ ilakkaṇamum, araṅkilē pukuntu āṭukiṉṟa muṟiyum, piṟa nuṭpaṅkaḷum cilappatikāra araṅkēṟṟu kātiyilē teḷivāka viḷakkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ikkuṟippukkaḷiyellām nōkkum poḻutu caṅka kālattilē tamiḻ makkaḷ vaḷartta iyalici nāṭakam eṉṟa muttamiḻum virinta pala tuṟikaḷiyuṭiyavi eṉpatu iṉitu pulaṉātal aṟika.
tamiḻ nāṭṭil ūrtōṟum iḷañciṟārkaṭkut toṭakkak kalviyiṉic collikkoṭukkum teṟṟip (tiṇṇip) paḷḷikkūṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. aṅkē iḷamāṇavar kaḷukku neṭuṅkaṇakku mutaliya aṭippaṭik kalviyiṉic collikkoṭukkum āciriyar palar iruntaṉar. aṅkup payilum māṇavarkaḷi `miyāṭalāṭal maḻa pulavar eṉap paripāṭal kūṟukiṉṟatu. iḷamparuva māṇavarkaḷip payiṟṟum āciriyarkaḷ `iḷampālāciriyar (toṭakkappaḷḷi āciriyar) eṉa vaḻaṅkappeṟṟārkaḷ. `maturi iḷampālāciriyaṉ cēntaṉ kūttaṉār eṉak kaṭiccaṅkap pulavaroruvarkku vaḻaṅkum peyarāl icceyti uṇarappaṭum. neṭuṅkaṇakku mutaliya toṭakkak kalviyiṉic collikkoṭukkum āciriyar `kaṇakkāyar eṉṟa peyarāl kuṟikkap peṟṟaṉar. uyarnta kalviyic collik koṭuppōr `āciriyar eṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟṟaṉar. ciṟuvarkaṭkuk kalviyiṉik kaṟpikkum poṟup piṉiyum celaviṉiyum peṟṟōrkaḷē ēṟṟuk koṇṭārkaḷ.
`tanti makaṟkāṟṟum naṉṟi aviyattu
munti yiruppac ceyal.    (kuṟaḷ. 69)
`cāṉṟō ṉākkutal tantikkuk kaṭaṉē.   (puṟam. 312)
eṉpavi īṇṭu niṉikkaṟpālaṉa. uyarnta kalviyiṉip peṟum viruppamuṭiyavar, āciriyarkku vēṇṭum utavikaḷic ceytum, perumporuḷ koṭuttum, avarkaḷiṭattup paṇivuṭiyavarāy oḻuki naṟporuḷkaḷi ōti uṇarntaṉar. akkālattut tamiḻ makkaḷ māṇava niliyil maṭṭumaṉṟi, maṇantukoṇṭa piṉṉarum, uyarnta nūṟporuḷkaḷik kaṟṟaṟkeṉavē vēṟṟūrkaḷukkup pirintu ceṉṟārkaḷ. iṅṅaṉam uyarnta kalviyiṉ poruṭṭup piriyum pirivu `ōtaṟpirivu eṉa akap poruḷ nūlkaḷiṟ ciṟappikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. ivvāṟu nāṭṭiluḷḷavar palarum tām cāntuṇiyum aṟivu nūlkaḷik kaṟṟuk kalviyiṟkaruttuṭiyavarāy vāḻntamiyāl, tamiḻ moḻi pala kalit tuṟikaḷilum ciṟappuṟṟu vaḷarvatāyiṟṟu.


VI
tamiḻar toḻil nili
oru nāṭṭiṉ kalvi nili uyarumāṉāl, ataṉ payaṉākap palvēṟu toḻilkaḷum tiruttampeṟṟu vaḷarcciyuṟutal eḷitākum. kalviyiṉ payaṉāka aṟivum, avvaṟiviṉ payaṉākat toḻilkaḷum vaḷarcciyaṭitalē vāḻkkiyiṉ vaḷarcci niliyākum.
tamiḻar tam aṟiviṉ tiṟattāl kaṇṭuṇarttiya palvēṟu kalikaḷ iviyeṉa muṉṉark kuṟikkap peṟṟaṉa.
vāḻkkikku iṉṟiyamiyātu vēṇṭap peṟuvatu uṇavu. nilamum nīruṅkūṭiya niliyilētāṉ ivvuṇaviṉi viḷivikka muṭiyum. nilatti naṉṟāka uḻutu puḻutiyākki, nīrpāycci, nel mutaliyavaṟṟi viḷivikkum payirttoḻili mēṟkoḷvār uḻavar eṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟuvar. `uḻattal eṉpatu iṭiviṭātu muyalum meymmuyaṟciyik kuṟikkum collākum. maḻi, paṉi, veyil eṉṉum kāla vēṟupāṭṭāl uṭampuk kuḷavākun tollikaḷi oru ciṟitum poruṭ paṭuttātu, neṟṟi vērvi nilattil viḻa iṭiviṭātu uḻikkum meymmuyaṟci ip payirt toḻilukku vēṇṭap paṭuvatākaliṉ, ittoḻili `uḻavu eṉṟa collāl paṇṭiyōr vaḻaṅkiṉar. uyir vāḻkkikku iṉṟiyamiyātu vēṇṭappeṟum ellāt toḻilkaḷilum ivvuḻavē talicciṟanta toḻil eṉpatu aṟiñar koḷki.
tamiḻar ellāt toḻiliṉum ivvuḻaviṉiyē taliyāka vittuc ciṟappittaṉar. ulaka makkaḷ tāṅkaḷ virumpiya palvēṟu toḻilkaḷiyuñceytu poruḷīṭṭiṉār kaḷāyiṉum, taṅkaḷ payiciṉit tīrttaṟkuriya uṇaviṉip peṟutal vēṇṭi uḻavaratu kiyiṉiyē etirpārttu niṟpar. meymmuyaṟciyuṭiya ivvuḻaviṉic ceytu uṇaviṉi uṇṭākkum āṟṟaliṉṟip piṟa toḻili mēṟkoḷvār ellāriyum uṇavaḷittup pātukāttalāl, ulakiyal vāḻvākiya tērkku accāṇi pōṉṟu utavi ceypavar ivvuḻavarēyāvar. yāvarum paci nīṅka uṇṇumpaṭi uḻutalic ceytu, ataṉāl tāmum uṇṭu, piṟar kiyi etirpārātu urimiyuṇarcciyuṭaṉ vāḻkki naṭattum tiṟamuṭiyār uḻavarē. ēṉiya toḻilāḷarellām piṟari yaṭuttu avartam ātaravu peṟṟu vāḻa vēṇṭiya eḷimi niliyiṉarē eṉpatu aṟiñar tuṇipu. piṟarpāṟ kiyēnti nillāta perumiyum, irappārkku illiyeṉṉātu vaḻaṅkum vaṇmiyum uḻavarkku iyalpāka aminta paṇpukaḷākum.
kuṟiyāta viḷiyuḷic ceyyum uḻavarkaḷ vāḻum nilappakutiyē nāṭeṉac ciṟappitturikkappaṭum. tam muyaṟciyāl viḷinta neṟkatir nīḻalil vāḻvārākiya uḻavar, tām viḷittuk koṭutta uṇaviṉ āṟṟalāl pala vēntar āṭciyilaminta nilapparappu muḻuvatiyum tam vēntaṉ āṭciyil aṭaṅkac ceyvar eṉpar.
`parappu nīrk kāvirip pāvitaṉ putalvar, irappōr cuṟṟamum purappōr koṟṟamum uḻaviṭi viḷippōr, eṉa iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ cōḻanāṭṭu uḻavarkaḷip pārāṭṭu kiṉṟār. nīriṉ tuṇikoṇṭu toḻil ceyvār uḻavarātaliṉ, avarkaḷip `parappunīrk kāvirip pāvitaṉ putalvar eṉa aṭikaḷ ciṟappittār. paciyāl varuntiyirakkum eḷiyāruṭiya cuṟṟattiyum, nāṭṭiṉik kākkum aracaratu veṟṟiyiyum, tam uḻaviṉiṭattē oru cēra viḷivikkut tiṟamiyuṭiyavar uḻavarākaliṉ, avarkaḷ vāḻum ūrkaḷip `paḻaviṟal ūrkaḷ eṉac cēra muṉivar pārāṭṭip pōṟṟiṉār. caṅkakālattil vāḻnta uḻavar, nilattiṉit tammuṭiyatākak koṇṭa kāṇiyāḷarāy viḷaṅkiṉamiyāl, eppoḻutu pārttālum tamakkuriya nilattiṉip paṇpaṭuttun toḻilil īṭupaṭṭiruntaṉar. oru palam niṟiyuḷḷa maṇṇāṉatu kāṟpalam niṟiyuḷḷa tākumpaṭi nilattiṉi āḻa uḻutu puḻutiyākkiṉāl, oru kippiṭi erukkūṭa nilattiṟku iṭṭaṉar. viḷi nilattilē payirukkut taṭiyāy vaḷarnta kaḷikaḷi avvappoḻutu kaḷinteṟintaṉar. curuṅkakkūṟiṉāl, payirttoḻiṟkuriya palavaki nuṭpaṅkaḷiyum tam toḻiṟ payiṟciyāl kaṇṭu payaṉpaṭuttiya perumi nam tamiḻ nāṭṭu uḻavarkaḷukku uriyateṉak kūṟalām. nāṭṭil makkaḷ toki perukap perukat tamakkuḷḷa ciṟiya nilattilē niṟinta uṇaviṉi viḷikka vēṇṭiya poṟuppu uḻavarkaḷukku uriyatāyiṟṟu. eṉavē, avarkaḷ tāṅkaḷ toṉṟutoṭṭup payiṉṟu varum attoḻili mēṉmēlum tiruttamuṟa vaḷarttu varat toṭaṅkiṉārkaḷ. ataṉāl, oru vēli nilattil āyirak kala nel viḷitaṟkēṟṟa vāyppu uṇṭāyiṟṟu. tāḻnta uṇarvuṭiya vilaṅkukaḷākiya erutukaḷi uḻaviṟ paḻakkip payir ceytu avaṟṟukku vēṇṭum vikkōl mutaliya uṇaviṉit tantu pātukākkum uḻavarkaḷip `pakaṭu puṟantarunar eṉap pulavar perumakkaḷ pārāṭṭip pōṟṟiṉārkaḷ. iṅṅaṉam taṉṉalaṅkarutātu piṟarkku utavi ceytal karuti iṭiviṭātu uḻikkum paṇpu ivvuḻavarkaḷiṭam ciṟappākak kāṇappaṭutal paṟṟi ivarkaḷi `vēḷāḷar eṉṟa collāṟ cāṉṟōr vaḻaṅkip pōṟṟiṉar eṉka (vēḷāḷar - piṟarkku utavupavar. vēḷāṇmi - upakāram.)
makkaḷukku iṉṟiyamiyātu vēṇṭappaṭuvaṉavaṟṟuḷ uṭiyum oṉṟākum. `nāṇuṭimi māntarciṟappu eṉṟār vaḷḷuvar. vilaṅkukaḷippōlat tiriyāmal, aṟṟam maṟittaṟ kuriya uṭiyiṉiyuṭuttu māṉattuṭaṉ vāḻum muṟi makkaḷ vāḻkkiyiṉ ciṟappiyalpākum. nākarikam vaḷarāta mikap paḻaṅ kālattē taḻiyiṉiyum, māṉ, puli mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ tōliṉi yum uṭuttu vāḻnta makkaḷ, taṅkaḷuṭiya nuṇṇaṟiviṉ tiṟattāl paruttiyiṉ pañciṉi nūlāka nūṟṟu āṭi neytu uṭuttuk koṇṭa ceyal, nākarikattiṉ taṉicciṟappākum. pallāyira āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē nam tamiḻ makkaḷ innecavut toḻiliṉik kaṇṭuṇarntu, ittoḻilil nuṇṇiya tērcci peṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ eṉpatu, paḻiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷāl naṉku tuṇiyappaṭum. makaḷir paruttip pañciṉik kaṭintu ceppañ ceytu nuṇṇiya nūlāka nūṟkun toḻiliṟ kittiṟam peṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷ nūṟṟu iḻittut tanta nūlip pāvāka virittut taṟiyil neytu āṭiyākak koṭukkum kaṭami āṭavar toḻilāy amintatu. parutti nūlālum, paṭṭu nūlālum, eli mayirālum nuṇṇiya āṭi kaḷi neypavarkaḷ tamiḻ nāṭṭup pērūrkaḷil āṅkāṅkē niṟipeṟṟut taṅkaḷ toḻiṟṟiṟattip perukkiṉārkaḷ. ivvāṟu necavut toḻilic ceytavar ` kārukar eṉṟa peyarāl akkālattu vaḻaṅkappeṟṟaṉar. ikkārukaviṉit toḻil mikavum nuṇṇuṇarvuṭaṉ ceytaṟkuriya tātaliṉ, itaṉi `nuṇviṉi eṉa iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ ciṟappittuḷḷār. akkālattu naṉṟākat tūymi ceyyappeṟṟa melliya nūlālum paṭṭiṉālum neyyap peṟṟa āṭikaḷ āviyip pōṉṟa meṉmiyum, kaṇṇiṉāl nōkki iḻikaḷip pirittuṇara muṭiyāta ceṟivum, kāṇpār kaṇ kavarum pūṅkarikaḷum, oḷiyum uṭiyaṉavāy, ellārālum virumpap peṟum aḻakiṉip peṟṟut tikaḻntamiyāl, `nōkku nuḻikallā nuṇmiya pūkkaṉintu aravuyaṉṉa aṟuvi, `āviyaṉṉa avirnūṟkaliṅkam, `kāmpu colittaṉṉa aṟuvi eṉap pulavar palarum pārāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. taṟiyil neytu aṟukkappaṭṭamiyāl, `aṟuvi eṉṟa peyar uṭikku uriyatāyiṟṟu. neyta uṭiyiṉic curukkamiṉṟi naṉṟāka maṭittu viṟṟu vantamiyāl, ataṟku `maṭi eṉṟa peyarum vaḻaṅkuva tāyiṟṟu.
